1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import os
     8→import shutil
     9→import sys
    10→from collections.abc import Iterator
    11→from contextlib import contextmanager
    12→from pathlib import Path
    13→from typing import cast
    14→
    15→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    16→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    18→    PLAN_VERSION,
    19→    PlanModel,
    20→    empty_plan,
    21→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    22→    validate_plan,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    25→    get_state_dir,
    26→    json_default,
    27→    utc_now,
    28→)
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→_PLAN_FILE_SENTINEL = object()
    33→PLAN_FILE = _PLAN_FILE_SENTINEL
    34→_INITIAL_PLAN_FILE = _PLAN_FILE_SENTINEL
    35→
    36→
    37→def get_plan_file() -> Path:
    38→    """Return the default plan file for the current runtime context."""
    39→    return get_state_dir() / "plan.json"
    40→
    41→
    42→def _default_plan_file() -> Path:
    43→    """Resolve the effective default plan path.
    44→
    45→    If tests monkeypatch ``PLAN_FILE`` in this module, use the patched value.
    46→    """
    47→    if PLAN_FILE != _INITIAL_PLAN_FILE:
    48→        return Path(PLAN_FILE)
    49→    return get_plan_file()
    50→
    51→
    52→@contextmanager
    53→def plan_lock(path: Path | None = None) -> Iterator[None]:
    54→    """Acquire exclusive lock on plan file for read-modify-write safety."""
    55→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
    56→    lock_path = plan_path.with_suffix(".lock")
    57→    lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    58→    fd = os.open(str(lock_path), os.O_CREAT | os.O_WRONLY)
    59→    try:
    60→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    61→            import msvcrt
    62→
    63→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_LOCK, 1)
    64→        else:
    65→            import fcntl
    66→
    67→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    68→        yield
    69→    finally:
    70→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    71→            import msvcrt
    72→
    73→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
    74→        else:
    75→            import fcntl
    76→
    77→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    78→        os.close(fd)
    79→
    80→
    81→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    82→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    83→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
    84→    if not plan_path.exists():
    85→        return empty_plan()
    86→
    87→    try:
    88→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    89→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    90→        # Try backup before giving up
    91→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    92→        if backup.exists():
    93→            try:
    94→                data = json.loads(backup.read_text())
    95→                logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s), loaded from backup.", ex)
    96→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.", file=sys.stderr)
    97→                # Fall through to validation below
    98→            except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as backup_ex:
    99→                logger.warning("Plan file and backup both corrupted: %s / %s", ex, backup_ex)
   100→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   101→                return empty_plan()
   102→        else:
   103→            logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
   104→            print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   105→            return empty_plan()
   106→
   107→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   108→        logger.warning("Plan file root is not a JSON object. Starting fresh.")
   109→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   110→        return empty_plan()
   111→
   112→    version = data.get("version", 1)
   113→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
   114→        logger.warning("Plan file version %d > supported %d.", version, PLAN_VERSION)
   115→        print(
   116→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
   117→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
   118→            file=sys.stderr,
   119→        )
   120→
   121→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
   122→    try:
   123→        validate_plan(data)
   124→    except ValueError as ex:
   125→        logger.warning("Plan invariants invalid (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
   126→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   127→        return empty_plan()
   128→
   129→    return cast(PlanModel, data)
   130→
   131→
   132→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   133→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
   134→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   135→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
   136→    validate_plan(plan)
   137→
   138→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
   139→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   140→
   141→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
   142→
   143→    if plan_path.exists():
   144→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
   145→        try:
   146→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
   147→        except OSError as backup_ex:
   148→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "create plan backup", backup_ex)
   149→
   150→    try:
   151→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
   152→    except OSError as ex:
   153→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   154→        raise
   155→
   156→
   157→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
   158→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
   159→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   160→
   161→
   162→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
   163→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
   164→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
   165→    if not plan_path.exists():
   166→        return False
   167→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   168→    return bool(
   169→        plan.get("queue_order")
   170→        or plan.get("overrides")
   171→        or plan.get("clusters")
   172→    )
   173→
   174→
   175→__all__ = [
   176→    "PLAN_FILE",
   177→    "get_plan_file",
   178→    "has_living_plan",
   179→    "load_plan",
   180→    "plan_lock",
   181→    "plan_path_for_state",
   182→    "save_plan",
   183→]
   184→

1→"""Post-import plan sync for review importing."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→import desloppify.engine.plan_queue as plan_queue_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_issue_id
    10→from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
    11→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    14→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE,
    15→    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→def _print_review_import_sync(
    20→    state: dict,
    21→    result: plan_queue_mod.ReviewImportSyncResult,
    22→    *,
    23→    workflow_injected: bool,
    24→) -> None:
    25→    """Print summary of plan changes after review import sync."""
    26→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    27→    stale_pruned = result.stale_pruned_from_queue
    28→    print()
    29→    if new_ids:
    30→        print(colorize(
    31→            f"  Plan updated: {len(new_ids)} new review issue(s) added to queue.",
    32→            "bold",
    33→        ))
    34→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
    35→        for finding_id in sorted(new_ids)[:10]:
    36→            finding = issues.get(finding_id, {})
    37→            print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(finding_id)}] {finding.get('summary', '')}")
    38→        if len(new_ids) > 10:
    39→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 10} more", "dim"))
    40→    if stale_pruned:
    41→        print(colorize(
    42→            f"  Plan updated: {len(stale_pruned)} stale review issue(s) removed from queue.",
    43→            "bold",
    44→        ))
    45→    print()
    46→    print(colorize(
    47→        "  Review queue sync completed. Workflow follow-up may be front-loaded.",
    48→        "dim",
    49→    ))
    50→    print()
    51→    print(colorize("  View queue:            desloppify plan queue", "dim"))
    52→    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
    53→    print()
    54→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP:", "yellow"))
    55→    print(colorize("    Run:    desloppify next", "yellow"))
    56→    if result.triage_injected and not workflow_injected:
    57→        print(colorize(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}", "dim"))
    58→        print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
    59→        print(colorize("    Manual dashboard: desloppify plan triage", "dim"))
    60→    print(colorize(
    61→        "  (Follow the queue in order; score communication and planning come before triage.)",
    62→        "dim",
    63→    ))
    64→
    65→
    66→def sync_plan_after_import(
    67→    state: dict,
    68→    diff: dict,
    69→    assessment_mode: str,
    70→    *,
    71→    state_file: str | Path | None = None,
    72→    config: dict | None = None,
    73→    import_file: str | None = None,
    74→    import_payload: dict | None = None,
    75→) -> None:
    76→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
    77→    try:
    78→        plan_path = None
    79→        if state_file is not None:
    80→            plan_path = plan_queue_mod.plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
    81→        if not plan_queue_mod.has_living_plan(plan_path):
    82→            return
    83→
    84→        plan = plan_queue_mod.load_plan(plan_path)
    85→        dirty = False
    86→        workflow_injected_ids: list[str] = []
    87→        policy = plan_queue_mod.compute_subjective_visibility(
    88→            state,
    89→            target_strict=target_strict_score_from_config(config),
    90→            plan=plan,
    91→        )
    92→
    93→        snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    94→        current_scores = plan_queue_mod.ScoreSnapshot(
    95→            strict=snapshot.strict,
    96→            overall=snapshot.overall,
    97→            objective=snapshot.objective,
    98→            verified=snapshot.verified,
    99→        )
   100→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   101→
   102→        communicate_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_communicate_score_needed(
   103→            plan,
   104→            state,
   105→            policy=policy,
   106→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
   107→            current_scores=current_scores,
   108→        )
   109→        if communicate_result.changes:
   110→            dirty = True
   111→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::communicate-score")
   112→
   113→        has_review_issue_delta = (
   114→            int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0) > 0
   115→            or int(diff.get("reopened", 0) or 0) > 0
   116→            or int(diff.get("auto_resolved", 0) or 0) > 0
   117→        )
   118→        import_result = None
   119→        covered_ids: list[str] = []
   120→        stale_sync_result = None
   121→
   122→        injected_parts: list[str] = []
   123→        if communicate_result.changes:
   124→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::communicate-score`")
   125→
   126→        import_scores_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_import_scores_needed(
   127→            plan,
   128→            state,
   129→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   130→            import_file=import_file,
   131→            import_payload=import_payload,
   132→        )
   133→        if import_scores_result.changes:
   134→            dirty = True
   135→            if import_scores_result.injected:
   136→                workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::import-scores")
   137→                injected_parts.append("`workflow::import-scores`")
   138→
   139→        create_plan_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_create_plan_needed(
   140→            plan,
   141→            state,
   142→            policy=policy,
   143→        )
   144→        if create_plan_result.changes:
   145→            dirty = True
   146→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::create-plan")
   147→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::create-plan`")
   148→
   149→        if has_review_issue_delta:
   150→            import_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_plan_after_review_import(
   151→                plan,
   152→                state,
   153→                policy=policy,
   154→            )
   155→            if import_result is not None:
   156→                dirty = True
   157→
   158→            # Keep stale/under-target subjective queue entries aligned with the
   159→            # latest imported assessments/issues. Importing review findings for
   160→            # one dimension must not globally evict other subjective dimensions.
   161→            stale_sync_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_stale_dimensions(
   162→                plan,
   163→                state,
   164→                policy=policy,
   165→            )
   166→            if stale_sync_result.changes:
   167→                dirty = True
   168→
   169→        if dirty:
   170→            if communicate_result.changes:
   171→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   172→                    plan,
   173→                    "sync_communicate_score",
   174→                    actor="system",
   175→                    detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   176→                )
   177→            if import_scores_result.changes:
   178→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   179→                    plan,
   180→                    "sync_import_scores",
   181→                    actor="system",
   182→                    detail={
   183→                        "trigger": "review_import",
   184→                        "injected": bool(import_scores_result.injected),
   185→                        "pruned": list(import_scores_result.pruned),
   186→                    },
   187→                )
   188→            if create_plan_result.changes:
   189→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   190→                    plan,
   191→                    "sync_create_plan",
   192→                    actor="system",
   193→                    detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   194→                )
   195→            if import_result is not None or workflow_injected_ids or covered_ids:
   196→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   197→                    plan,
   198→                    "review_import_sync",
   199→                    actor="system",
   200→                    detail={
   201→                        "trigger": "review_import",
   202→                        "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   203→                        "added_to_queue": (
   204→                            import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else []
   205→                        ),
   206→                        "workflow_injected_ids": workflow_injected_ids,
   207→                        "triage_injected": (
   208→                            import_result.triage_injected if import_result is not None else False
   209→                        ),
   210→                        "triage_injected_ids": (
   211→                            import_result.triage_injected_ids if import_result is not None else []
   212→                        ),
   213→                        "triage_deferred": (
   214→                            import_result.triage_deferred if import_result is not None else False
   215→                        ),
   216→                        "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   217→                        "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   218→                        "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   219→                        "stale_pruned_from_queue": (
   220→                            import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue if import_result is not None else []
   221→                        ),
   222→                        "covered_subjective": covered_ids,
   223→                        "stale_sync_injected": (
   224→                            sorted(stale_sync_result.injected)
   225→                            if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   226→                        ),
   227→                        "stale_sync_pruned": (
   228→                            sorted(stale_sync_result.pruned)
   229→                            if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   230→                        ),
   231→                    },
   232→                )
   233→            plan_queue_mod.save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   234→
   235→        if import_result is not None:
   236→            _print_review_import_sync(
   237→                state,
   238→                import_result,
   239→                workflow_injected=bool(workflow_injected_ids),
   240→            )
   241→        if injected_parts:
   242→            print(colorize(
   243→                f"  Plan: {' and '.join(injected_parts)} queued. Run `desloppify next`.",
   244→                "cyan",
   245→            ))
   246→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:

1→"""Lifecycle visibility filtering for work-queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.plan_queue import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
     7→
     8→
     9→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    10→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue.
    11→
    12→    Checks both individual issues and collapsed clusters — clusters
    13→    contain objective issues grouped by the queue builder.
    14→    """
    15→    return any(
    16→        item.get("kind") in ("issue", "cluster")
    17→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    18→        for item in items
    19→    )
    20→
    21→
    22→def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    23→    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
    24→    return any(
    25→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    26→        and item.get("initial_review")
    27→        for item in items
    28→    )
    29→
    30→
    31→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    32→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
    33→    if item.get("detector") == "subjective_review":
    34→        return True
    35→    return (
    36→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    37→        and not item.get("initial_review")
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→def _has_endgame_subjective(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    42→    """True if any non-initial subjective review items are pending."""
    43→    return any(_is_endgame_only(item) for item in items)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _has_triage_stages(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    47→    """True if any pending triage stage items are in the queue."""
    48→    return any(
    49→        item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
    50→        and str(item.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
    51→        for item in items
    52→    )
    53→
    54→
    55→def _is_triage_stage(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    56→    """True when item is a triage workflow stage."""
    57→    return (
    58→        item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
    59→        and str(item.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→
    63→def _is_force_visible(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    64→    """True when the item is explicitly escalated past objective gating."""
    65→    return bool(item.get("force_visible"))
    66→
    67→
    68→def apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    69→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules."""
    70→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
    71→        return [
    72→            item for item in items
    73→            if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review")
    74→        ]
    75→    # Enforce fixed endgame phase order:
    76→    #   subjective review -> score workflow -> triage
    77→    # When non-initial subjective reruns are pending and objective work is
    78→    # already drained, keep focus on subjective reruns and defer workflow/triage.
    79→    if _has_endgame_subjective(items) and not _has_objective_items(items):
    80→        return [
    81→            item for item in items
    82→            if _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item)
    83→        ]
    84→    if _has_triage_stages(items):
    85→        # Triage should not block while objective queue work still exists.
    86→        if _has_objective_items(items):
    87→            return [
    88→                item for item in items
    89→                if (
    90→                    (not _is_triage_stage(item) or _is_force_visible(item))
    91→                    and (not _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item))
    92→                )
    93→            ]
    94→        return [
    95→            item for item in items
    96→            if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    97→        ]
    98→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
    99→        return items
   100→    return [item for item in items if not _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item)]
   101→
   102→
   103→__all__ = ["apply_lifecycle_filter"]
   104→

60→def resolve_plan_load_status(
    61→    *,
    62→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    63→) -> PlanLoadStatus:
    64→    """Resolve plan loading and explicitly report degraded mode."""
    65→    if not isinstance(plan, _PlanAutoLoad):
    66→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    67→
    68→    if not has_living_plan():
    69→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=None, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    70→
    71→    try:
    72→        resolved_plan = load_plan()
    73→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    74→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    75→            plan=None,
    76→            degraded=True,
    77→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    78→        )
    79→    return PlanLoadStatus(plan=resolved_plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)

1→"""Shared constants and helpers for plan internals."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
     9→
    10→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    11→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    12→
    13→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    14→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    15→    "triage::observe",
    16→    "triage::reflect",
    17→    "triage::organize",
    18→    "triage::enrich",
    19→    "triage::sense-check",
    20→    "triage::commit",
    21→)
    22→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    23→_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES = {
    24→    stage_id.removeprefix("triage::") for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    25→}
    26→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    27→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    28→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    29→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    30→WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID = "workflow::deferred-disposition"
    31→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    32→WORKFLOW_IDS = {
    33→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    34→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    35→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    36→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    37→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    38→}
    39→WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER = (
    40→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    41→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    42→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    43→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    44→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    45→)
    46→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    47→
    48→
    49→@dataclass
    50→class QueueSyncResult:
    51→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    52→
    53→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    56→    deferred: bool = False
    57→
    58→    @property
    59→    def changes(self) -> int:
    60→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)
    61→
    62→
    63→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    64→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    65→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    66→        return {}
    67→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    68→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    69→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    70→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    71→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    72→        return meta_or_stages
    73→    return {}
    74→
    75→
    76→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
    78→    return {
    79→        str(name)
    80→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    81→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
    82→    }
    83→
    84→
    85→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
    87→    return {
    88→        str(name)
    89→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    90→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
    91→    }
    92→
    93→
    94→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
    95→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
    96→    seen: set[str] = set()
    97→    normalized: list[str] = []
    98→
    99→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   100→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   101→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   102→            seen.add(issue_id)
   103→
   104→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   105→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   106→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   107→            seen.add(issue_id)
   108→
   109→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   110→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   111→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   112→            seen.add(issue_id)
   113→
   114→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   115→        if issue_id in seen:
   116→            continue
   117→        normalized.append(issue_id)
   118→        seen.add(issue_id)
   119→
   120→    queue_order[:] = normalized
   121→
   122→
   123→__all__ = [
   124→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   125→    "QueueSyncResult",
   126→    "normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix",
   127→    "confirmed_triage_stage_names",
   128→    "recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names",

    52→        for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
    53→    ]
    54→    if manual_fallback:

    56→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    57→    return tools
    58→
    59→
    60→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    61→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    62→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    63→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    64→    for stage, _sid in _TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    65→        stage_payload = triage_stages.get(stage)
    66→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    67→            return stage, True

    69→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    70→    for stage, sid in _TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    71→        if sid not in order:

    73→        return stage, False
    74→
    75→    if "triage::commit" in order:

    91→    if stage == "commit":

    93→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)

   261→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   262→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   263→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   264→        return []
   265→    deferred: list[str] = []
   266→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   267→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   268→            continue
   269→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   270→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   271→    deferred.sort()
   272→    return deferred
   273→
   274→
   275→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   276→    plan: dict,
   277→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   278→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   279→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   280→    if not deferred_set:
   281→        return 0, 0
   282→
   283→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   284→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   285→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   286→
   287→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   288→    cluster_count = 0
   289→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   290→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   291→            continue
   292→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   293→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   294→            continue
   295→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   296→        if not matched:
   297→            continue
   298→        cluster_count += 1
   299→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   300→
   301→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   302→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   303→
   304→
   305→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   306→    """Build a synthetic item prompting deferred backlog disposition."""
   307→    deferred_ids = _temporary_skipped_ids(plan)
   308→    if not deferred_ids:
   309→        return None
   310→
   311→    count = len(deferred_ids)
   312→    cluster_count, individual_count = _deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)
   313→    cluster_label = "cluster" if cluster_count == 1 else "clusters"
   314→    individual_label = "item" if individual_count == 1 else "items"
   315→    reactivate_cmd = 'desloppify plan unskip "*"'
   316→    subset_reactivate_cmd = "desloppify plan unskip <cluster-or-id>"
   317→    inspect_cmd = "desloppify plan queue --include-skipped"
   318→    inspect_item_cmd = "desloppify show <cluster-or-id>"
   319→    wontfix_cmd = (
   320→        'desloppify plan skip --permanent "*" '
   321→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   322→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently." '
   323→        "--confirm"
   324→    )
   325→    subset_wontfix_cmd = (
   326→        "desloppify plan skip --permanent <cluster-or-id> "
   327→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   328→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently."'
   329→    )
   330→
   331→    return {
   332→        "id": WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
   333→        "tier": 1,
   334→        "confidence": "high",
   335→        "detector": "workflow",
   336→        "file": ".",
   337→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   338→        "summary": (
   339→            "Deferred backlog decision required: "
   340→            f"{cluster_count} {cluster_label} + {individual_count} individual {individual_label} "
   341→            "must be reactivated or marked wontfix."
   342→        ),
   343→        "detail": {
   344→            "temporary_skipped_count": count,
   345→            "deferred_cluster_count": cluster_count,
   346→            "deferred_individual_count": individual_count,
   347→            "reactivate_command": reactivate_cmd,
   348→            "wontfix_command": wontfix_cmd,
   349→            "planning_tools": [
   350→                {
   351→                    "label": "Review deferred backlog",
   352→                    "command": inspect_cmd,
   353→                },
   354→                {
   355→                    "label": "Inspect a specific cluster or item",
   356→                    "command": inspect_item_cmd,
   357→                },
   358→                {
   359→                    "label": "Reactivate a subset",
   360→                    "command": subset_reactivate_cmd,
   361→                },
   362→                {
   363→                    "label": "Mark a subset as permanent wontfix",
   364→                    "command": subset_wontfix_cmd,
   365→                },
   366→            ],
   367→            "decision_options": [
   368→                {
   369→                    "label": "Reactivate deferred work",
   370→                    "command": reactivate_cmd,
   371→                },
   372→                {
   373→                    "label": "Convert deferred work to wontfix",
   374→                    "command": wontfix_cmd,
   375→                },
   376→            ],
   377→        },
   378→        "primary_command": reactivate_cmd,
   379→        "blocked_by": [],
   380→        "is_blocked": False,
   381→    }

1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
    14→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→
    19→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    20→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    21→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    22→})
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    27→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    28→
    29→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    30→    objective_count: int  # how many
    31→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    32→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    33→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    34→
    35→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    36→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    37→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    38→            return True  # unconditional
    39→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    40→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    41→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    42→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    43→        return False
    44→
    45→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    46→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    47→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    48→            return False  # never evict unscored
    49→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    50→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    51→        return False
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    55→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    56→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    57→
    58→
    59→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    61→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    62→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    63→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    64→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    65→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    66→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    67→            return True
    68→    return False
    69→
    70→
    71→class _ScanPathFromStatePolicy:
    72→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    73→
    74→
    75→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = _ScanPathFromStatePolicy()
    76→ScanPathPolicyOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromStatePolicy
    77→
    78→
    79→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    83→    scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    84→    plan: dict | None = None,
    85→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    86→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    87→
    88→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    89→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    90→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    91→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    92→
    93→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    94→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import (
    97→        current_stale_ids,
    98→        current_under_target_ids,
    99→        current_unscored_ids,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   103→        state.get("scan_path")
   104→        if isinstance(scan_path, _ScanPathFromStatePolicy)
   105→        else scan_path
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   109→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   110→
   111→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   112→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   113→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   114→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   115→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   116→    objective_count = sum(
   117→        1
   118→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   119→        if issue.get("status") == Status.OPEN
   120→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   121→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   122→        and not _is_evidence_only(issue)
   123→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(issue.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   124→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   128→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   129→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   132→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   133→        objective_count=objective_count,
   134→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   135→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   136→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   142→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   143→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   144→]
   145→
```

+def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
+    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
+    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
+
+
+def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
+    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
+    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
+    if not plan_path.exists():
+        return False
+    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
+    return bool(
+        plan.get("queue_order")
+        or plan.get("deferred")
+        or plan.get("overrides")
+        or plan.get("clusters")
+    )
+
+
+__all__ = [
+    "PLAN_FILE",
--
+    "plan_path_for_state",
+    "save_plan",
+]

    29→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:

    51→    for entry in reversed(audit):

   135→

   154→    current_packet_path = _normalize_match_path(provenance_dict.get("packet_path")) or ""
   155→    if expected_packet_path and current_packet_path != expected_packet_path:
   156→        mismatches.append(

   159→        )
   160→
   161→    expected_dims = meta.get("assessment_dimensions") or []
   162→    if expected_dims:

   172→            )
   173→    return not mismatches, mismatches

   176→def _clear_pending_import_scores(plan: PlanModel) -> None:

   178→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   179→        order[:] = [item for item in order if item != WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]
   180→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)

   188→    """Return True when no unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain."""
   189→    if policy is not None:

   191→    return not stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
   192→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,

   196→def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
   197→    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
   198→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   199→    if item_id not in order:
   200→        order.append(item_id)
   201→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   202→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   203→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   204→        skipped.pop(item_id, None)
   205→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=[item_id])
   206→

   208→_EMPTY = QueueSyncResult

   211→def sync_score_checkpoint_needed(
   212→    plan: PlanModel,
   213→    state: StateModel,
   214→    *,
   215→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   216→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   217→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   218→
   219→    Injects when:
   220→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   221→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   222→
   223→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   224→    """
   225→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   226→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   227→
   228→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   229→        return _EMPTY()
   230→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   231→        return _EMPTY()
   232→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
   233→
   234→
   235→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   236→    plan: PlanModel,
   237→    state: StateModel,
   238→    *,
   239→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   240→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   241→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   242→
   243→    Only injects when:
   244→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   245→    - At least one objective issue exists
   246→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   247→    - No triage stages are pending
   248→
   249→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   250→    """
   251→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   252→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   253→
   254→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   255→        return _EMPTY()
   256→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   257→        return _EMPTY()
   258→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   259→        return _EMPTY()
   260→
   261→    if not has_objective_backlog(state, policy):
   262→        return _EMPTY()
   263→
   264→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   265→
   266→
   267→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   268→    plan: PlanModel,
   269→    state: StateModel,
   270→    *,
   271→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   272→    import_file: str | None = None,
   273→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   274→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   275→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   276→
   277→    Only injects when:
   278→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   279→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   280→
   281→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   282→    """
   283→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   284→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   285→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   286→    pending_meta = refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   287→    latest_issues_only = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   288→    latest_trusted = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes=_TRUSTED_ASSESSMENT_MODES)
   289→
   290→    stale_pending = False
   291→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   292→        if latest_issues_only is None:
   293→            stale_pending = True
   294→        elif latest_trusted is not None:
   295→            latest_issues_ts = str(latest_issues_only.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   296→            latest_trusted_ts = str(latest_trusted.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   297→            pending_ts = ""
   298→            if isinstance(pending_meta, dict):
   299→                pending_ts = str(pending_meta.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   300→            compare_ts = pending_ts or latest_issues_ts
   301→            if compare_ts and latest_trusted_ts and latest_trusted_ts >= compare_ts:
   302→                stale_pending = True
   303→    if stale_pending:
   304→        _clear_pending_import_scores(plan)
   305→        return QueueSyncResult(pruned=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   306→
   307→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   308→        if assessment_mode == "issues_only":
   309→            # Update metadata to track the latest issues-only batch
   310→            refresh_state[_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY] = _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   311→                import_file=import_file,
   312→                import_payload=import_payload,
   313→                issues_only_audit=latest_issues_only,
   314→            )
   315→            return QueueSyncResult(resurfaced=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   316→        return _EMPTY()
   317→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   318→        return _EMPTY()
   319→    result = _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   320→    refresh_state[_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY] = _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   321→        import_file=import_file,
   322→        import_payload=import_payload,
   323→        issues_only_audit=latest_issues_only,
   324→    )
   325→    return result
   326→
   327→
   328→class ScoreSnapshot:
   329→    """Minimal score snapshot for rebaseline — avoids importing state.py."""
   330→
   331→    __slots__ = ("strict", "overall", "objective", "verified")
   332→
   333→    def __init__(
   334→        self,
   335→        *,
   336→        strict: float | None,
   337→        overall: float | None,
   338→        objective: float | None,
   339→        verified: float | None,
   340→    ) -> None:
   341→        self.strict = strict
   342→        self.overall = overall
   343→        self.objective = objective
   344→        self.verified = verified
   345→
   346→
   347→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   348→    plan: PlanModel,
   349→    state: StateModel,
   350→    *,
   351→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   352→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   353→    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
   354→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   355→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` and rebaseline scores.
   356→
   357→    Injects when:
   358→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dims), OR
   359→      scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   360→    - ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue
   361→    - Score has not already been communicated this cycle
   362→      (``previous_plan_start_scores`` absent), unless a trusted score import
   363→      explicitly refreshed the live score mid-cycle
   364→
   365→    When injected and *current_scores* is provided, ``plan_start_scores``
   366→    is rebaselined to the current score so the score display unfreezes at
   367→    the new value.  The previous baseline is preserved in
   368→    ``previous_plan_start_scores`` so the communicate-score queue item can
   369→    show the old → new delta — and so mid-cycle scans know not to
   370→    re-inject.
   371→    """
   372→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   373→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   374→
   375→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order:
   376→        return _EMPTY()
   377→    # Already communicated this cycle — previous_plan_start_scores is set
   378→    # at injection time and cleared at cycle boundaries.
   379→    if "previous_plan_start_scores" in plan and not scores_just_imported:
   380→        return _EMPTY()
   381→    if not scores_just_imported and not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   382→        return _EMPTY()
   383→
   384→    if current_scores is not None:
   385→        _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(plan, current_scores)
   386→    # Set sentinel even when rebaseline was a no-op (no plan_start_scores
   387→    # to rebaseline) so mid-cycle scans don't re-inject.
   388→    if not plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores"):
   389→        plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {}
   390→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   391→
   392→
   393→def _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(
   394→    plan: PlanModel,
   395→    scores: ScoreSnapshot,
   396→) -> None:
   397→    """Snapshot the current score as the new baseline, preserving the old one."""
   398→    old_start = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   399→    if not isinstance(old_start, dict) or not old_start:
   400→        return
   401→    if scores.strict is None:
   402→        return
   403→
   404→    plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = dict(old_start)
   405→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   406→        "strict": scores.strict,
   407→        "overall": scores.overall,
   408→        "objective": scores.objective,
   409→        "verified": scores.verified,
   410→    }
   411→
   412→
   413→__all__ = [
   414→    "ScoreSnapshot",
   415→    "import_scores_meta_matches",
   416→    "pending_import_scores_meta",
   417→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   418→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   419→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   420→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   421→]
   422→
```

    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→# Dimension key normalization
    47→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48→
    49→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    50→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    51→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    52→    target = cleaned.lower()
    53→
    54→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    55→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    56→            return str(dim_key)
    57→    return slugify(cleaned)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    61→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    62→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    63→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    64→    return {
    65→        cleaned.lower(),
    66→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    67→        slugify(cleaned),
    68→        canonical.lower(),
    69→        slugify(canonical),
    70→    }
    71→
    72→
    73→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    74→# Subjective strict scores
    75→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76→
    77→def subjective_strict_scores(state: StateModel | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, float]:
    78→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    79→    if not dim_scores:
    80→        return {}
    81→
    82→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    83→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    84→    for entry in entries:
    85→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    86→        if not name:
    87→            continue
    88→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    89→        [REDACTED](name)
    90→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    91→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    92→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    93→            if not key:
    94→                continue
    95→            aliases.add(key)
    96→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    97→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    98→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    99→        for alias in aliases:
   100→            scores[alias] = strict_val

    43→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    44→
    45→
    46→class _ScanPathFromState:
    47→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    48→
    49→
    50→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    51→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    52→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = _ScanPathFromState()
    53→ScanPathOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromState
    54→
    55→
    56→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    57→class QueueBuildOptions:
    58→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    59→
    60→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    61→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    62→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    63→    """
    64→
    65→    # Output control
    66→    count: int | None = 1
    67→    explain: bool = False
    68→
    69→    # Scope filtering
    70→    scan_path: ScanPathOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    71→    scope: str | None = None
    72→    status: str = "open"
    73→    chronic: bool = False
    74→
    75→    # Subjective gating
    76→    include_subjective: bool = True
    77→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    78→
    79→    # Plan integration
    80→    plan: dict | None = None
    81→    include_skipped: bool = False
    82→
    83→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    84→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    85→
    86→
    87→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    88→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    89→
    90→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    91→    total: int
    92→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    93→    new_ids: set[str]
    94→
    95→
    96→def build_work_queue(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   100→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   101→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
   102→
   103→    Pipeline:
   104→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
   105→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom
   106→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
   107→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   108→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   109→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   110→    """
   111→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   112→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   113→
   114→    # 1. Gather
   115→    items = build_issue_items(
   116→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   117→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   118→    )
   119→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   120→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   121→
   122→    # 2. Score
   123→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   124→
   125→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   126→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   127→
   128→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   129→    items = apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   130→
   131→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   132→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   133→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   134→
   135→    # 6. Finalize
   136→    if not items:
   137→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   138→    total = len(items)
   139→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   140→        items = items[:opts.count]
   141→    if opts.explain:
   142→        for item in items:
   143→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   144→
   145→    return {
   146→        "items": items,
   147→        "total": total,
   148→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   149→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   150→    }
   151→
   152→
   153→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   154→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   155→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   156→
   157→
   158→def _resolve_inputs(
   159→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   160→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   161→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   162→    ctx = opts.context
   163→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   164→
   165→    scan_path: str | None = (
   166→        state.get("scan_path")
   167→        if isinstance(opts.scan_path, _ScanPathFromState)
   168→        else opts.scan_path
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    status = opts.status
   172→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   173→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   174→
   175→    try:
   176→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   177→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   178→        threshold = 100.0
   179→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   180→
   181→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   182→
   183→
   184→def _gather_subjective_items(
   185→    state: StateModel,
   186→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   187→    threshold: float,
   188→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   189→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   190→
   191→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in ``apply_lifecycle_filter``,
   192→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   193→    """
   194→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   195→        return []
   196→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   197→        return []
   198→    if opts.chronic:
   199→        return []
   200→
   201→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   202→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   203→    )
   204→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   205→
   206→
   207→def _gather_workflow_items(
   208→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   209→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   210→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   211→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   212→        return []
   213→
   214→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   215→    for builder in (
   216→        build_score_checkpoint_item,
   217→        build_import_scores_item,
   218→        build_communicate_score_item,
   219→    ):
   220→        item = builder(plan, state)
   221→        if item is not None:
   222→            items.append(item)
   223→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   224→    if plan_item is not None:
   225→        items.append(plan_item)
   226→    return items
   227→
   228→
   229→
   230→def _plan_presort(
   231→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   232→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   233→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   234→
   235→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   236→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   237→    """
   238→    if not plan:
   239→        return set(), []
   240→
   241→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   242→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   243→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   244→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   245→    items[:] = remaining
   246→    return new_ids, skipped
   247→
   248→
   249→def _plan_postsort(
   250→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   251→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
   252→    plan: dict | None,
   253→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   254→) -> None:
   255→    """Re-append skipped items and stamp positions.
   256→
   257→    Cluster focus filtering is intentionally NOT applied here — it is a
   258→    view-layer concern that callers apply after building the canonical queue.
   259→    This prevents UI focus state from affecting lifecycle decisions (scan
   260→    gating, score display mode, empty-queue fallback).
   261→    """
   262→    if not plan:
   263→        return
   264→
   265→    if opts.include_skipped:
   266→        items.extend(skipped)
   267→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   268→
   269→
   270→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   271→    """Return end-of-queue workflow actions when no active queue items remain."""
   272→    if not plan:
   273→        return []
   274→
   275→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   276→    deferred_item = build_deferred_disposition_item(plan)
   277→    if deferred_item is not None:
   278→        items.append(deferred_item)
   279→        return items
   280→
   281→    if not postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   282→        return items
   283→
   284→    items.append({
   285→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   286→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   287→        "summary": "Queue cleared - run scan to refresh and surface follow-up review work.",
   288→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   289→        "file": "",
   290→        "detector": "workflow",
   291→        "confidence": "high",
   292→    })
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
   296→__all__ = [
   297→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   298→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   299→    "QueueContext",
   300→    "WorkQueueResult",
   301→    "build_work_queue",
   302→    "collapse_clusters",
   303→    "group_queue_items",
   304→]

"""Post-import plan sync for review importing."""

from __future__ import annotations

import desloppify.engine.plan_queue as plan_queue_mod
from desloppify import state as state_mod
from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_issue_id
from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE,
    TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX,
)


def _print_review_import_sync(
    state: dict,
    result: plan_queue_mod.ReviewImportSyncResult,
    *,
    workflow_injected: bool,
) -> None:
    """Print summary of plan changes after review import sync."""
    new_ids = result.new_ids
    stale_pruned = result.stale_pruned_from_queue
    print()
    if new_ids:
        print(colorize(
            f"  Plan updated: {len(new_ids)} new review issue(s) added to queue.",
            "bold",
        ))
        issues = state.get("issues", {})
        for finding_id in sorted(new_ids)[:10]:
            finding = issues.get(finding_id, {})
            print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(finding_id)}] {finding.get('summary', '')}")
        if len(new_ids) > 10:
            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 10} more", "dim"))
    if stale_pruned:
        print(colorize(
            f"  Plan updated: {len(stale_pruned)} stale review issue(s) removed from queue.",
            "bold",
        ))
    print()
    print(colorize(
        "  Review queue sync completed. Workflow follow-up may be front-loaded.",
        "dim",
    ))
    print()
    print(colorize("  View queue:            desloppify plan queue", "dim"))
    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
    print()
    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP:", "yellow"))
    print(colorize("    Run:    desloppify next", "yellow"))
    if result.triage_injected and not workflow_injected:
        print(colorize(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}", "dim"))
        print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
        print(colorize("    Manual dashboard: desloppify plan triage", "dim"))
    print(colorize(
        "  (Follow the queue in order; score communication and planning come before triage.)",
        "dim",
    ))


def sync_plan_after_import(
    state: dict,
    diff: dict,
    assessment_mode: str,
    *,
    config: dict | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
    try:
        if not plan_queue_mod.has_living_plan():
            return

        plan = plan_queue_mod.load_plan()
        dirty = False
        workflow_injected_ids: list[str] = []
        policy = plan_queue_mod.compute_subjective_visibility(
            state,
