```
1→"""Workflow-specific synthetic work queue item builders."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import shlex
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan_queue import (
     8→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
     9→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    10→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    11→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    12→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    13→    pending_import_scores_meta,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (
    16→    triage_manual_stage_command,
    17→    triage_run_stages_command,
    18→    triage_runner_commands,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    21→
    22→_TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (
    23→    ("observe", "triage::observe"),
    24→    ("reflect", "triage::reflect"),
    25→    ("organize", "triage::organize"),
    26→    ("enrich", "triage::enrich"),
    27→    ("sense-check", "triage::sense-check"),
    28→)
    29→
    30→
    31→def _confirm_attestation_hint(stage: str) -> str:
    32→    if stage == "observe":
    33→        return "I have thoroughly reviewed..."
    34→    if stage == "reflect":
    35→        return "My strategy accounts for..."
    36→    if stage == "organize":
    37→        return "This plan is correct..."
    38→    if stage == "enrich":
    39→        return "Steps are executor-ready..."
    40→    if stage == "sense-check":
    41→        return "Content and structure verified..."
    42→    return "..."
    43→
    44→
    45→def _runner_planning_tools(
    46→    *,
    47→    only_stages: str | None = None,
    48→    manual_fallback: str | None = None,
    49→) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    50→    tools = [
    51→        {"label": label, "command": command}
    52→        for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
    53→    ]
    54→    if manual_fallback:
    55→        tools.append({"label": "Manual fallback", "command": manual_fallback})
    56→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    57→    return tools
    58→
    59→
    60→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    61→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    62→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    63→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    64→    for stage, _sid in _TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    65→        stage_payload = triage_stages.get(stage)
    66→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    67→            return stage, True
    68→
    69→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    70→    for stage, sid in _TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    71→        if sid not in order:
    72→            continue
    73→        return stage, False
    74→
    75→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    76→        return "commit", False
    77→
    78→    return None, False
    79→
    80→
    81→def _create_plan_primary_command(plan: dict) -> str:
    82→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
    83→    if stage is None:
    84→        return triage_run_stages_command()
    85→    if needs_confirm:
    86→        attestation = _confirm_attestation_hint(stage)
    87→        return (
    88→            f'desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage} '
    89→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'
    90→        )
    91→    if stage == "commit":
    92→        return triage_run_stages_command()
    93→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)
    94→
    95→
    96→def _create_plan_planning_tools(plan: dict) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    97→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
    98→    if needs_confirm:
    99→        return []
   100→    if stage == "commit":
   101→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   102→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("commit"),
   103→        )
   104→    if stage:
   105→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   106→            only_stages=stage,
   107→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command(stage),
   108→        )
   109→    return _runner_planning_tools(
   110→        only_stages="observe",
   111→        manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   112→    )
   113→
   114→
   115→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   116→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
   117→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   118→        return None
   119→
   120→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   121→
   122→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   123→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   124→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   125→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   126→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   127→
   128→    return {
   129→        "id": WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
   130→        "tier": 1,
   131→        "confidence": "high",
   132→        "detector": "workflow",
   133→        "file": ".",
   134→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   135→        "summary": f"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   136→        "detail": {
   137→            "strict": strict,
   138→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   139→            "delta": delta,
   140→            "planning_tools": _runner_planning_tools(
   141→                only_stages="observe",
   142→                manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   143→            ),
   144→        },
   145→        "primary_command": triage_run_stages_command(only_stages="observe"),
   146→        "blocked_by": [],
   147→        "is_blocked": False,
   148→    }
   149→
   150→
   151→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   152→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if queued."""
   153→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   154→        return None
   155→
   156→    return {
   157→        "id": WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   158→        "tier": 1,
   159→        "confidence": "high",
   160→        "detector": "workflow",
   161→        "file": ".",
   162→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   163→        "summary": "Create prioritized plan from review results",
   164→        "detail": {
   165→            "planning_tools": _create_plan_planning_tools(plan),
   166→        },
   167→        "primary_command": _create_plan_primary_command(plan),
   168→        "blocked_by": [],
   169→        "is_blocked": False,
   170→    }
   171→
   172→
   173→def build_import_scores_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   174→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::import-scores`` if queued."""
   175→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   176→        return None
   177→    meta = pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state) or {}
   178→    import_file = str(meta.get("import_file", "")).strip() or "issues.json"
   179→    quoted_import_file = shlex.quote(import_file)
   180→    packet_sha = str(meta.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   181→    packet_path = str(meta.get("packet_path", "")).strip()
   182→    dims = meta.get("assessment_dimensions") or []
   183→    explanation = (
   184→        "Review issues were imported but assessment scores were skipped "
   185→        "(untrusted source). Re-import the same batch with attestation to update dimension scores."
   186→    )
   187→    if import_file != "issues.json":
   188→        explanation += f" Expected import file: `{import_file}`."
   189→    if packet_sha:
   190→        explanation += f" Expected packet sha256: `{packet_sha}`."
   191→    if packet_path:
   192→        explanation += f" Blind packet: `{packet_path}`."
   193→    if dims:
   194→        explanation += f" Assessment dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}."
   195→
   196→    return {
   197→        "id": WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
   198→        "tier": 1,
   199→        "confidence": "high",
   200→        "detector": "workflow",
   201→        "file": ".",
   202→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   203→        "summary": "Import assessment scores with attestation",
   204→        "detail": {
   205→            "explanation": explanation,
   206→            "expected_import_file": import_file,
   207→            "packet_sha256": packet_sha,
   208→            "packet_path": packet_path,
   209→            "assessment_dimensions": dims,
   210→        },
   211→        "primary_command": (
   212→            f"desloppify review --import {quoted_import_file} --attested-external "
   213→            '--attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness '
   214→            'of overall score and is unbiased."'
   215→        ),
   216→        "blocked_by": [],
   217→        "is_blocked": False,
   218→    }
   219→
   220→
   221→def build_communicate_score_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   222→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::communicate-score`` if queued."""
   223→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   224→        return None
   225→
   226→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   227→
   228→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   229→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   230→
   231→    # Use previous_plan_start_scores (stashed at rebaseline) for the delta
   232→    # so the user sees old-baseline → current, not current → current.
   233→    prev_start = (plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   234→    if prev_start is None:
   235→        prev_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   236→    delta = round(strict - prev_start, 1) if prev_start is not None else None
   237→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   238→
   239→    return {
   240→        "id": WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   241→        "tier": 1,
   242→        "confidence": "high",
   243→        "detector": "workflow",
   244→        "file": ".",
   245→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   246→        "summary": f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   247→        "detail": {
   248→            "strict": strict,
   249→            "previous_plan_start_strict": prev_start,
   250→            "delta": delta,
   251→        },
   252→        "primary_command": (
   253→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   254→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   255→        ),
   256→        "blocked_by": [],
   257→        "is_blocked": False,
   258→    }
   259→
   260→
   261→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   262→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   263→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   264→        return []
   265→    deferred: list[str] = []
   266→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   267→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   268→            continue
   269→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   270→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   271→    deferred.sort()
   272→    return deferred
   273→
   274→
   275→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   276→    plan: dict,
   277→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   278→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   279→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   280→    if not deferred_set:
   281→        return 0, 0
   282→
   283→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   284→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   285→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   286→
   287→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   288→    cluster_count = 0
   289→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   290→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   291→            continue
   292→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   293→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   294→            continue
   295→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   296→        if not matched:
   297→            continue
   298→        cluster_count += 1
   299→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   300→
   301→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   302→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   303→
   304→
   305→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   306→    """Build a synthetic item prompting deferred backlog disposition."""
   307→    deferred_ids = _temporary_skipped_ids(plan)
   308→    if not deferred_ids:
   309→        return None
   310→
   311→    count = len(deferred_ids)
   312→    cluster_count, individual_count = _deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)
   313→    cluster_label = "cluster" if cluster_count == 1 else "clusters"
   314→    individual_label = "item" if individual_count == 1 else "items"
   315→    reactivate_cmd = 'desloppify plan unskip "*"'
   316→    subset_reactivate_cmd = "desloppify plan unskip <cluster-or-id>"
   317→    inspect_cmd = "desloppify plan queue --include-skipped"
   318→    inspect_item_cmd = "desloppify show <cluster-or-id>"
   319→    wontfix_cmd = (
   320→        'desloppify plan skip --permanent "*" '
   321→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   322→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently." '
   323→        "--confirm"
   324→    )
   325→    subset_wontfix_cmd = (
   326→        "desloppify plan skip --permanent <cluster-or-id> "
   327→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   328→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently."'
   329→    )
   330→
   331→    return {
   332→        "id": WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
   333→        "tier": 1,
   334→        "confidence": "high",
   335→        "detector": "workflow",
   336→        "file": ".",
   337→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   338→        "summary": (
   339→            "Deferred backlog decision required: "
   340→            f"{cluster_count} {cluster_label} + {individual_count} individual {individual_label} "
   341→            "must be reactivated or marked wontfix."
   342→        ),
   343→        "detail": {
   344→            "temporary_skipped_count": count,
   345→            "deferred_cluster_count": cluster_count,
   346→            "deferred_individual_count": individual_count,
   347→            "reactivate_command": reactivate_cmd,
   348→            "wontfix_command": wontfix_cmd,
   349→            "planning_tools": [
   350→                {
   351→                    "label": "Review deferred backlog",
   352→                    "command": inspect_cmd,
   353→                },
   354→                {
   355→                    "label": "Inspect a specific cluster or item",
   356→                    "command": inspect_item_cmd,
   357→                },
   358→                {
   359→                    "label": "Reactivate a subset",
   360→                    "command": subset_reactivate_cmd,
   361→                },
   362→                {
   363→                    "label": "Mark a subset as permanent wontfix",
   364→                    "command": subset_wontfix_cmd,
   365→                },
   366→            ],
   367→            "decision_options": [
   368→                {
   369→                    "label": "Reactivate deferred work",
   370→                    "command": reactivate_cmd,
   371→                },
   372→                {
   373→                    "label": "Convert deferred work to wontfix",
   374→                    "command": wontfix_cmd,
   375→                },
   376→            ],
   377→        },
   378→        "primary_command": reactivate_cmd,
   379→        "blocked_by": [],
   380→        "is_blocked": False,
   381→    }
   382→
   383→
   384→__all__ = [
   385→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   386→    "build_create_plan_item",
   387→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   388→    "build_import_scores_item",
   389→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   390→]
   391→
```

```
1→"""Shared constants and helpers for plan internals."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
     9→
    10→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    11→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    12→
    13→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    14→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    15→    "triage::observe",
    16→    "triage::reflect",
    17→    "triage::organize",
    18→    "triage::enrich",
    19→    "triage::sense-check",
    20→    "triage::commit",
    21→)
    22→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    23→_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES = {
    24→    stage_id.removeprefix("triage::") for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    25→}
    26→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    27→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    28→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    29→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    30→WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID = "workflow::deferred-disposition"
    31→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    32→WORKFLOW_IDS = {
    33→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    34→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    35→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    36→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    37→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    38→}
    39→WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER = (
    40→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    41→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    42→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    43→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    44→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    45→)
    46→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    47→
    48→
    49→@dataclass
    50→class QueueSyncResult:
    51→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    52→
    53→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    56→    deferred: bool = False
    57→
    58→    @property
    59→    def changes(self) -> int:
    60→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)
    61→
    62→
    63→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    64→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    65→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    66→        return {}
    67→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    68→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    69→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    70→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    71→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    72→        return meta_or_stages
    73→    return {}
    74→
    75→
    76→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
    78→    return {
    79→        str(name)
    80→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    81→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
    82→    }
    83→
    84→
    85→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
    87→    return {
    88→        str(name)
    89→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    90→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
    91→    }
    92→
    93→
    94→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
    95→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
    96→    seen: set[str] = set()
    97→    normalized: list[str] = []
    98→
    99→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   100→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   101→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   102→            seen.add(issue_id)
   103→
   104→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   105→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   106→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   107→            seen.add(issue_id)
   108→
   109→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   110→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   111→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   112→            seen.add(issue_id)
   113→
   114→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   115→        if issue_id in seen:
   116→            continue
   117→        normalized.append(issue_id)
   118→        seen.add(issue_id)
   119→
   120→    queue_order[:] = normalized
   121→
   122→
   123→__all__ = [
   124→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   125→    "QueueSyncResult",
   126→    "normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix",
   127→    "confirmed_triage_stage_names",
   128→    "recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names",
   129→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   130→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
```

```
1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan_ops import (
     9→    get_issue_description,
    10→    get_issue_note,
    11→    get_issue_override,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    14→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    18→    """Return issue IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    19→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    20→    if not scan_history:
    21→        return set()
    22→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    23→    if not threshold:
    24→        return set()
    25→    return {
    26→        issue_id
    27→        for issue_id, issue in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    28→        if issue.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    29→    }
    30→
    31→
    32→def enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
    33→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    34→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    35→
    36→    for item in items:
    37→        issue_id = item["id"]
    38→        description = get_issue_description(plan, issue_id)
    39→        note = get_issue_note(plan, issue_id)
    40→        override = get_issue_override(plan, issue_id)
    41→        if description:
    42→            item["plan_description"] = description
    43→        if note:
    44→            item["plan_note"] = note
    45→        if override.get("cluster"):
    46→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    47→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    48→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    49→                "name": cluster_name,
    50→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    51→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", [])),
    52→                "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
    53→            }
    54→
    55→
    56→def stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    57→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    58→    plan: dict,
    59→    new_ids: set[str],
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    62→
    63→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    64→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    65→    """
    66→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    67→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    68→    subjective_defer_meta = plan.get("subjective_defer_meta", {})
    69→    if not isinstance(subjective_defer_meta, dict):
    70→        subjective_defer_meta = {}
    71→    force_visible_ids = {
    72→        str(fid).strip()
    73→        for fid in subjective_defer_meta.get("force_visible_ids", [])
    74→        if str(fid).strip()
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    78→    for idx, issue_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    79→        if issue_id not in skipped_ids:
    80→            position_map[issue_id] = idx
    81→
    82→    for item in items:
    83→        item_id = item["id"]
    84→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    85→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    86→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    87→        if item_id in force_visible_ids:
    88→            item["force_visible"] = True
    89→
    90→
    91→def separate_skipped(
    92→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
    93→    plan: dict,
    94→) -> tuple[list[WorkQueueItem], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
    95→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    96→
    97→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    98→    """
    99→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   100→    if not skipped_ids:
   101→        return items, []
   102→    non_skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   103→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   104→    for item in items:
   105→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   106→            skipped.append(item)
   107→        else:
   108→            non_skipped.append(item)
   109→    return non_skipped, skipped
   110→
   111→
   112→def filter_cluster_focus(
   113→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   114→    plan: dict,
   115→    cluster: str | None,
   116→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   117→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
   118→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
   119→    if not effective_cluster:
   120→        return items
   121→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   122→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   123→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   124→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   125→        return items
   126→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   127→
   128→
   129→def stamp_positions(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> None:
   130→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   131→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   132→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   133→
   134→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   135→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   136→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   137→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   138→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   139→            if skip_entry:
   140→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   141→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   142→                if skip_reason:
   143→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   144→
   145→
   146→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   147→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   148→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   149→    if meta:
   150→        return meta.action_type
   151→    return "manual_fix"
   152→
   153→
   154→def _build_cluster_meta(
   155→    cluster_name: str, members: list[WorkQueueItem], cluster_data: dict[str, Any]
   156→) -> WorkQueueItem:
   157→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   158→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   159→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   160→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   161→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   162→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   163→        action_type = "reorganize"
   164→    else:
   165→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   166→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   167→            action_type = "refactor"
   168→
   169→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   170→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("issue_ids", []))
   171→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   172→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   173→    else:
   174→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} issues"
   175→
   176→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   177→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   178→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   179→        autofix_hint = action
   180→    else:
   181→        primary_command = action or f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   182→        autofix_hint = None
   183→
   184→    estimated_impact = max(
   185→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   186→    )
   187→
   188→    return {
   189→        "id": cluster_name,
   190→        "kind": "cluster",
   191→        "action_type": action_type,
   192→        "summary": summary,
   193→        "members": members,
   194→        "member_count": len(members),
   195→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   196→        "autofix_hint": autofix_hint,
   197→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   198→        "cluster_auto": bool(cluster_data.get("auto")),
   199→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   200→        "confidence": "high",
   201→        "detector": detector,
   202→        "file": "",
   203→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   204→        "action_steps": cluster_data.get("action_steps") or [],
   205→    }
   206→
   207→
   208→def collapse_clusters(items: list[WorkQueueItem], plan: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   209→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   210→
   211→    Both auto-clusters and manual (triage) clusters are collapsed.  Manual
   212→    clusters are inserted at the front in plan order so triage-prioritised
   213→    work appears before auto-clustered mechanical items.
   214→    """
   215→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   216→    if not clusters:
   217→        return items
   218→
   219→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   220→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   221→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
   222→            # Manual clusters take priority when an issue is in both
   223→            if issue_id not in fid_to_cluster or not cluster.get("auto"):
   224→                fid_to_cluster[issue_id] = name
   225→
   226→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   227→        return items
   228→
   229→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   230→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]] = {}
   231→    for item in items:
   232→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   233→        if cname:
   234→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   235→
   236→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   237→    meta_items: dict[str, WorkQueueItem] = {}
   238→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   239→        if len(members) < 2:
   240→            continue
   241→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   242→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   243→        )
   244→
   245→    # Collect manual cluster names in plan order (for front-insertion)
   246→    manual_names = [
   247→        name for name in clusters
   248→        if not clusters[name].get("auto") and name in meta_items
   249→    ]
   250→
   251→    # Walk in order: replace first auto-cluster member with meta-item,
   252→    # skip subsequent members.  Manual cluster members are always skipped
   253→    # (they'll be inserted at the front).
   254→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   255→    rest: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   256→    for item in items:
   257→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   258→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   259→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   260→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   261→                # Auto-clusters collapse in-place; manual clusters go to front
   262→                if clusters.get(cname, {}).get("auto"):
   263→                    rest.append(meta_items[cname])
   264→            # skip individual member
   265→        else:
   266→            rest.append(item)
   267→
   268→    # Manual clusters at the front in plan order, then everything else
   269→    manual_result = [meta_items[name] for name in manual_names if name in seen_clusters]
   270→    return manual_result + rest
   271→
   272→
   273→__all__ = [
   274→    "collapse_clusters",
   275→    "enrich_plan_metadata",
   276→    "filter_cluster_focus",
   277→    "new_item_ids",
   278→    "separate_skipped",
   279→    "stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   280→    "stamp_positions",
   281→]
   282→
```

```
   108→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   109→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   110→
   111→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   112→    """
   113→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   114→    order_set = set(order)
   115→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   116→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   117→    force_visible = bool(meta.get("triage_force_visible"))
   118→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   119→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
   121→    present_names = {
   122→        name
   123→        for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False)
   124→        if sid in present_ids
   125→    }
   126→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   127→    if not present_names:
   128→        return []
   129→
   130→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   131→    open_review_count = sum(
   132→        1 for f in issues.values()
   133→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   138→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   139→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   140→        if name not in present_names:
   141→            continue
   142→        if name in confirmed:
   143→            continue
   144→
   145→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   146→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   147→        blocked_by = sorted(
   148→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   149→        )
   150→
   151→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   152→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   153→
   154→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   155→            "id": sid,
   156→            "tier": 1,
   157→            "confidence": "high",
   158→            "detector": "triage",
   159→            "file": ".",
   160→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   161→            "force_visible": force_visible,
   162→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   163→            "detail": {
   164→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   165→                "stage": name,
   166→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   167→                "runner_commands": [
   168→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   169→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   170→                ],
   171→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   172→            },
   173→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   174→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   175→        }
   176→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   177→        items.append(item)
   178→    return items
   179→

   181→def build_subjective_items(
   182→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   183→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   184→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   185→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   186→    if not dim_scores:
   187→        return []
   188→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   189→
   190→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   191→    if not subjective_entries:
   192→        return []
   193→    unassessed_dims = {
   194→        str(name).strip()
   195→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   196→            dim_scores
   197→        )
   198→    }
   199→
   200→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   201→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   202→    for issue in issues.values():
   203→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   204→            continue
   205→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   206→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   207→            if dim_key:
   208→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   209→
   210→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   211→    def _prepare_command(
   212→        cli_keys: list[str],
   213→        *,
   214→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   215→    ) -> str:
   216→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   217→        if cli_keys:
   218→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   219→        if force_review_rerun:
   220→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   221→        return command
   222→
   223→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   224→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   225→        if not name:
   226→            continue
   227→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   228→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   229→            continue
   230→
   231→        [REDACTED](name)
   232→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   233→        cli_keys = [
   234→            str(key).strip().lower()
   235→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   236→            if str(key).strip()
   237→        ]
   238→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   239→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   240→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   241→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   242→            name in unassessed_dims
   243→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   244→        )
   245→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   246→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   247→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   248→        if open_review > 0:
   249→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   250→        else:
   251→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   252→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   253→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   254→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   255→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   256→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   257→            "file": ".",
   258→            "confidence": "medium",
   259→            "summary": summary,
   260→            "detail": {
   261→                "dimension_name": name,
   262→                "dimension": dim_key,
   263→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   264→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   265→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   266→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   267→            },
   268→            "status": "open",
   269→        }
   270→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   271→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   272→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   273→        items.append(item)
   274→    return items
```

```
    49→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    50→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    51→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    52→    target = cleaned.lower()
    53→
    54→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    55→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    56→            return str(dim_key)
    57→    return slugify(cleaned)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    61→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with issue dimension keys."""
    62→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    63→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    64→    return {
    65→        cleaned.lower(),
    66→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    67→        slugify(cleaned),
    68→        canonical.lower(),
    69→        slugify(canonical),
    70→    }

    89→        [REDACTED](name)

    94→                continue
    95→            aliases.add(key)
    96→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    97→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    98→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    99→        for alias in aliases:
   100→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   101→    return scores

   107→
   108→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   109→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   110→
   111→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   112→    """
   113→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   114→    order_set = set(order)
   115→    present_ids = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   116→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   117→    force_visible = bool(meta.get("triage_force_visible"))
   118→    confirmed = confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   119→    recorded_unconfirmed = recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta)
   120→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "enrich", "sense-check", "commit")
   121→    present_names = {
   122→        name
   123→        for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False)
   124→        if sid in present_ids
   125→    }
   126→    present_names.update(recorded_unconfirmed)
   127→    if not present_names:
   128→        return []
   129→
   130→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   131→    open_review_count = sum(
   132→        1 for f in issues.values()
   133→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   134→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   135→    )
   136→
   137→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   138→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   139→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names, strict=False):
   140→        if name not in present_names:
   141→            continue
   142→        if name in confirmed:
   143→            continue
   144→
   145→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   146→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   147→        blocked_by = sorted(
   148→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps if dep in present_names and dep not in confirmed
   149→        )
   150→
   151→        only_stages = None if name == "commit" else name
   152→        cmd = triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=only_stages)
   153→
   154→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   155→            "id": sid,
   156→            "tier": 1,
   157→            "confidence": "high",
   158→            "detector": "triage",
   159→            "file": ".",
   160→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   161→            "force_visible": force_visible,
   162→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   163→            "detail": {
   164→                "total_review_issues": open_review_count,
   165→                "stage": name,
   166→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   167→                "runner_commands": [
   168→                    {"label": label, "command": command}
   169→                    for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
   170→                ],
   171→                "manual_fallback": triage_manual_stage_command(name),
   172→            },
   173→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   174→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   175→        }
   176→        item["primary_command"] = cmd
   177→        items.append(item)
   178→    return items
   179→
   180→
   181→def build_subjective_items(
   182→    state: dict, issues: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   183→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   184→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   185→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   186→    if not dim_scores:
   187→        return []
   188→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   189→
   190→    subjective_entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   191→    if not subjective_entries:
   192→        return []
   193→    unassessed_dims = {
   194→        str(name).strip()
   195→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   196→            dim_scores
   197→        )
   198→    }
   199→
   200→    # Review issues are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   201→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   202→    for issue in issues.values():
   203→        if issue.get("status") != "open":
   204→            continue
   205→        if issue.get("detector") == "review":
   206→            dim_key = str(detail_dict(issue).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   207→            if dim_key:
   208→                review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   209→
   210→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   211→    def _prepare_command(
   212→        cli_keys: list[str],
   213→        *,
   214→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   215→    ) -> str:
   216→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   217→        if cli_keys:
   218→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   219→        if force_review_rerun:
   220→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   221→        return command
   222→
   223→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   224→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   225→        if not name:
   226→            continue
   227→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   228→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   229→            continue
   230→
   231→        [REDACTED](name)
   232→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   233→        cli_keys = [
   234→            str(key).strip().lower()
   235→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   236→            if str(key).strip()
   237→        ]
   238→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   239→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   240→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   241→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   242→            name in unassessed_dims
   243→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("failing", 0)) == 0)
   244→        )
   245→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   246→        # If review issues already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   247→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   248→        if open_review > 0:
   249→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   250→        else:
   251→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   252→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   253→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   254→        item: WorkQueueItem = {
   255→            "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   256→            "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   257→            "file": ".",
   258→            "confidence": "medium",
   259→            "summary": summary,
   260→            "detail": {
   261→                "dimension_name": name,
   262→                "dimension": dim_key,
   263→                "failing": int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
   264→                "strict_score": strict_val,
   265→                "open_review_issues": open_review,
   266→                "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   267→            },
   268→            "status": "open",
   269→        }
   270→        item["kind"] = "subjective_dimension"
   271→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command
   272→        item["initial_review"] = is_unassessed
   273→        items.append(item)
   274→    return items
   275→
```

```
1→"""Subjective code review: context building, file selection, and issue import.
     2→
     3→Desloppify prepares structured review data (context + file batches + prompts)
     4→for an AI agent to evaluate. The agent returns structured issues that are
     5→imported back into state like any other detector.
     6→
     7→Ownership boundary:
     8→- ``app.commands.review`` owns workflow orchestration and CLI policy.
     9→- ``intelligence.review`` owns domain helpers (context, selection, prepare,
    10→  import, remediation) with no command orchestration.
    11→
    12→No LLM calls happen here — this package is pure Python domain logic.
    13→"""
    14→
    15→from pathlib import Path
    16→from typing import Any
    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    19→from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.contracts_types import ReviewImportPayload

    25→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,

    28→from desloppify.intelligence.review.context_holistic.orchestrator import (
    29→    build_holistic_context,

    31→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import load_dimensions_for_lang

    33→    DIMENSION_PROMPTS,

    36→)

    38→    HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG,
    39→    LANG_GUIDANCE,
    40→    get_lang_guidance,
    41→)
    42→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.selection import resolve_dimensions
    43→from desloppify.intelligence.review.policy import (
    44→    DimensionPolicy,
    45→    append_custom_dimensions,
    46→    build_dimension_policy,
    47→    filter_assessments_for_scoring,
    48→    is_allowed_dimension,

    52→from desloppify.intelligence.review.prepare import (

    58→from desloppify.intelligence.review.prepare_batches_builders import build_investigation_batches
    59→from desloppify.intelligence.review.remediation import generate_remediation_plan
    60→from desloppify.intelligence.review.selection import (
    61→    LOW_VALUE_NAMES,

    66→
    67→
    68→def import_review_issues(
    69→    issues_data: ReviewImportPayload,
    70→    state: StateModel,
    71→    lang_name: str,
    72→    *,
    73→    project_root: Path | str | None = None,
    74→    utc_now_fn=utc_now,
    75→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    76→    """Lazy wrapper to avoid import cycles during package initialization."""
    77→    from desloppify.intelligence.review.importing.per_file import (
    78→        import_review_issues as _import_review_issues,
    79→    )
    80→
    81→    return _import_review_issues(
    82→        issues_data,
    83→        state,
    84→        lang_name,
    85→        project_root=project_root,
    86→        utc_now_fn=utc_now_fn,
    87→    )
```

```
1→"""Lightweight integrity helpers for subjective/review scoring.
     2→
     3→This module intentionally lives outside ``desloppify.intelligence.review`` so command/state
     4→paths can import it without loading the heavier review package.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
    10→
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
    12→    SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
    13→    matches_target_score,
    14→)
    15→
    16→__all__ = [
    17→    "SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE",
    18→    "is_holistic_subjective_issue",
    19→    "is_subjective_review_open",
    20→    "matches_target_score",
    21→    "subjective_review_open_breakdown",
    22→    "unassessed_subjective_dimensions",
    23→]
    24→
    25→
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# Internal iteration helper
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→
    31→def _iter_issues(
    32→    issues: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    33→) -> Iterable[tuple[str, dict]]:
    34→    """Yield (issue_id, issue) pairs from mapping or iterable inputs."""
    35→    if isinstance(issues, Mapping):
    36→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items():
    37→            if isinstance(issue, dict):
    38→                yield str(issue_id), issue
    39→        return
    40→
    41→    for index, issue in enumerate(issues):
    42→        if isinstance(issue, dict):
    43→            yield str(index), issue
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Public helpers (formerly in integrity/review.py)
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→
    51→def is_subjective_review_open(issue: dict) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True when a issue is an open subjective-review signal."""
    53→    return (
    54→        issue.get("status") == "open"
    55→        and issue.get("detector") == "subjective_review"
    56→    )
    57→
    58→
    59→def is_holistic_subjective_issue(issue: dict, *, issue_id: str = "") -> bool:
    60→    """Best-effort check for holistic subjective-review coverage issues."""
    61→    candidate_id = str(issue.get("id") or issue_id or "")
    62→    if "::holistic_unreviewed" in candidate_id or "::holistic_stale" in candidate_id:
    63→        return True
    64→
    65→    summary = str(issue.get("summary", "") or "").lower()
    66→    if "holistic" in summary and "review" in summary:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    70→    return bool(detail.get("holistic"))
    71→
    72→
    73→def subjective_review_open_breakdown(
    74→    issues: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    75→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    76→    """Return open subjective count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    77→    reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    78→    holistic_reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    79→    total = 0
    80→
    81→    for issue_id, issue in _iter_issues(issues):
    82→        if not is_subjective_review_open(issue):
    83→            continue
    84→
    85→        total += 1
    86→        reason = str(issue.get("detail", {}).get("reason", "other") or "other")
    87→        reason_counts[reason] = reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    88→
    89→        if is_holistic_subjective_issue(issue, issue_id=issue_id):
    90→            holistic_reason_counts[reason] = holistic_reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    91→
    92→    return total, reason_counts, holistic_reason_counts
    93→
    94→
    95→def unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    96→    """Return subjective dimension display names that are still 0% placeholders."""
    97→    if not dim_scores:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    unassessed: list[str] = []
   101→    for name, info in dim_scores.items():
   102→        detectors = info.get("detectors", {})
   103→        if "subjective_assessment" not in detectors:
   104→            continue
   105→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
   106→        if isinstance(assessment_meta, dict) and assessment_meta.get("placeholder"):
   107→            unassessed.append(name)
   108→            continue
   109→        strict_val = float(info.get("strict", info.get("score", 100.0)))
   110→        issues = int(info.get("failing", 0))
   111→        if strict_val <= 0.0 and issues == 0:
   112→            unassessed.append(name)
   113→
   114→    unassessed.sort()
   115→    return unassessed
   116→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from typing import Any, cast
     7→
     8→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    10→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    14→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
    15→    detail_dict,
    16→    is_review_issue,
    17→    is_subjective_issue,
    18→    primary_command_for_issue,
    19→    review_issue_weight,
    20→    scope_matches,
    21→    slugify,
    22→    status_matches,
    23→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    24→    workflow_stage_name,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking_output import (
    27→    group_queue_items,
    28→    item_explain,
    29→    subjective_score_value,
    30→)
    31→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import subjective_strict_scores
    32→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    33→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    34→
    35→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    36→
    37→# Plan-aware sort tiers (item_sort_key)
    38→_TIER_PLANNED = 0   # Items with explicit plan position
    39→_TIER_EXISTING = 1  # Known items, natural ranking
    40→_TIER_NEW = 2       # Newly discovered items
    41→
    42→# Natural ranking groups (_natural_sort_key)
    43→_RANK_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3  # Unassessed subjective dimensions
    44→_RANK_WORKFLOW = -2         # Score checkpoints, create-plan
    45→_RANK_TRIAGE_STAGE = -1     # Epic triage workflow stages
    46→_RANK_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered issues
    47→_RANK_ISSUE = 1           # Individual issues + assessed subjective
    48→
    49→_TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER = {
    50→    "observe": 0,
    51→    "reflect": 1,
    52→    "organize": 2,
    53→    "commit": 3,
    54→}
    55→
    56→
    57→def _workflow_stage_index(item: WorkQueueItem) -> int:
    58→    raw_index = item.get("stage_index")
    59→    if raw_index is not None:
    60→        if isinstance(raw_index, int):
    61→            return raw_index
    62→        if isinstance(raw_index, str):
    63→            stripped = raw_index.strip()
    64→            if stripped.lstrip("-").isdigit():
    65→                return int(stripped)
    66→        logger.warning("Invalid workflow stage index %r", raw_index)
    67→    return _TRIAGE_STAGE_ORDER.get(workflow_stage_name(item).lower(), 0)
    68→
    69→
    70→def enrich_with_impact(
    71→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]
    72→) -> None:
    73→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    74→
    75→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    76→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    77→    """
    78→    if not dimension_scores:
    79→        for item in items:
    80→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    81→        return
    82→
    83→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key="strict")
    84→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    85→
    86→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    87→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    88→    for entry in entries:
    89→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    90→        if not name:
    91→            continue
    92→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    93→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    94→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    95→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    96→
    97→    for item in items:
    98→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    99→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
   100→
   101→
   102→def _compute_item_impact(
   103→    item: WorkQueueItem,
   104→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
   105→    get_dimension_for_detector,
   106→) -> float:
   107→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
   108→    kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   109→
   110→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective issues):
   111→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
   112→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   113→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
   114→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
   115→        if entry:
   116→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   117→        return 0.0
   118→
   119→    # Mechanical issues: use detector -> dimension mapping
   120→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   121→    if detector:
   122→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   123→        if dimension:
   124→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
   125→            if entry:
   126→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
   127→
   128→    return 0.0
   129→
   130→
   131→def build_issue_items(
   132→    state: StateModel,
   133→    *,
   134→    scan_path: str | None,
   135→    status_filter: str,
   136→    scope: str | None,
   137→    chronic: bool,
   138→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   139→    scoped = path_scoped_issues(state.get("issues", {}), scan_path)
   140→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   141→    out: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   142→
   143→    for issue_id, issue in scoped.items():
   144→        if issue.get("suppressed"):
   145→            continue
   146→        if not status_matches(issue.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   147→            continue
   148→        if chronic and not (
   149→            issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   150→        ):
```

```
     5→
     6→Between scans, items are completed via ``purge_ids`` (what ``plan resolve``
     7→does) and the queue is re-checked without reconciling.  ``reconcile`` only

    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→# State / plan helpers
    47→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48→
    49→def _placeholder_dim_entries(key: str) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    50→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) for a placeholder."""
    51→    return (
    52→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    53→        {
    54→            "score": 0, "strict": 0, "failing": 0, "checks": 0,
    55→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": True, "dimension_key": key}},
    56→        },
    57→        {"score": 0, "placeholder": True},
    58→    )
    59→
    60→
    61→def _scored_dim_entries(key: str, score: float) -> tuple[str, dict, dict]:
    62→    """(display_name, dim_scores_entry, subjective_assessment) with a real score."""
    63→    return (
    64→        DIM_DISPLAY[key],
    65→        {
    66→            "score": score, "strict": score, "failing": 0, "checks": 1,
    67→            "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"placeholder": False, "dimension_key": key}},
    68→        },
    69→        {"score": score, "placeholder": False},
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _build_state(
    74→    issues: list[dict],
    75→    dims: list[tuple[str, dict, dict]],
    76→    *,
    77→    strict_score: float | None = None,
    78→    overall_score: float | None = None,
    79→    objective_score: float | None = None,
    80→) -> dict:
    81→    state: dict = {
    82→        "issues": {i["id"]: dict(i) for i in issues},
    83→        "scan_count": 1,
    84→        "dimension_scores": {},
    85→        "subjective_assessments": {},
    86→    }
    87→    for display, dim_entry, assessment in dims:
    88→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
    89→        dim_key = dim_entry["detectors"]["subjective_assessment"]["dimension_key"]
    90→        state["subjective_assessments"][dim_key] = assessment
    91→    if strict_score is not None:
    92→        state["strict_score"] = strict_score
    93→    if overall_score is not None:
    94→        state["overall_score"] = overall_score
    95→    if objective_score is not None:
    96→        state["objective_score"] = objective_score
    97→    return state
    98→
    99→
   100→def _reconcile(state: dict, plan: dict, monkeypatch) -> dict:
   101→    """Simulate a scan boundary: run reconcile_plan_post_scan with mocked I/O."""
   102→    saved: list[dict] = []
   103→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "load_plan", lambda _path=None: plan)
   104→    monkeypatch.setattr(reconcile_mod, "save_plan", lambda p, _path=None: saved.append(p))
   105→    reconcile_mod.reconcile_plan_post_scan(SimpleNamespace(
   106→        state=state, state_path=Path("/tmp/fake-state.json"), config={},
   107→    ))
   108→    return saved[-1] if saved else plan
   109→
   110→
   111→def _queue_ids(state: dict, plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   112→    result = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(plan=plan, count=None))
   113→    return [item["id"] for item in result["items"]]
   114→
   115→
   116→def _spoof_reviews_complete(state: dict) -> None:
   117→    """Mutate state in place: replace all placeholder dims with scored ones."""
   118→    for key in DIM_KEYS:
   119→        display, dim_entry, assessment = _scored_dim_entries(key, 75.0)
   120→        state["dimension_scores"][display] = dim_entry
   121→        state["subjective_assessments"][key] = assessment
   122→    state["strict_score"] = 75.0
   123→    state["overall_score"] = 75.0
   124→    state["objective_score"] = 80.0
   125→
   126→
   127→def _complete_endgame_subjective_reruns(state: dict) -> None:
   128→    """Mark scored subjective dimensions as fully current/high to clear rerun queue."""
   129→    for key in DIM_KEYS:
   130→        assessment = state["subjective_assessments"].setdefault(key, {})
   131→        assessment["placeholder"] = False
   132→        assessment["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   133→        assessment["score"] = 100.0
   134→        dim = state["dimension_scores"][DIM_DISPLAY[key]]
   135→        dim["score"] = 100.0
   136→        dim["strict"] = 100.0
   137→
   138→
   139→def _add_review_issues(state: dict) -> None:
   140→    """Mutate state in place: add review detector issues that trigger triage."""
   141→    for key in ("naming_quality", "logic_clarity"):
   142→        fid = f"review-{key}"
   143→        state["issues"][fid] = {
   144→            "id": fid, "detector": "review", "file": "src/app.py",
   145→            "status": "open", "detail": {"dimension": key},
   146→        }
   147→
   148→
   149→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→# Test 1: Fresh scan — only initial reviews visible
```

```
    29→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
    30→    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
    31→    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
    32→        refresh_state = {}
    33→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    34→    return refresh_state
    35→
    36→
    37→def _normalize_match_path(raw_path: object) -> str | None:
    38→    if not isinstance(raw_path, str) or not raw_path.strip():
    39→        return None
    40→    return str(Path(raw_path).expanduser().resolve(strict=False))
    41→
    42→
    43→def _latest_assessment_audit(
    44→    state: StateModel,
    45→    *,
    46→    modes: set[str],
    47→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    48→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    49→    if not isinstance(audit, list):
    50→        return None
    51→    for entry in reversed(audit):
    52→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    53→            continue
    54→        mode = entry.get("mode")
    55→        if isinstance(mode, str) and mode in modes:
    56→            return entry
    57→    return None
    58→
    59→
    60→def _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
    61→    *,
    62→    import_file: str | None,
    63→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None,
    64→    issues_only_audit: dict[str, Any] | None,
    65→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    66→    provenance = {}
    67→    assessments = {}
    68→    issues = []
    69→    if isinstance(import_payload, dict):
    70→        raw_provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
    71→        if isinstance(raw_provenance, dict):
    72→            provenance = raw_provenance
    73→        raw_assessments = import_payload.get("assessments")
    74→        if isinstance(raw_assessments, dict):
    75→            assessments = raw_assessments
    76→        raw_issues = import_payload.get("issues")
    77→        if isinstance(raw_issues, list):
    78→            issues = raw_issues
    79→    recorded_file = (
    80→        str(import_file).strip()
    81→        if isinstance(import_file, str) and import_file.strip()
    82→        else str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")).strip()
    83→        if isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict)
    84→        else ""
    85→    )
    86→    timestamp = ""
    87→    if isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict):
    88→        timestamp = str(issues_only_audit.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
    89→    return {
    90→        "timestamp": timestamp,
    91→        "import_file": recorded_file,
    92→        "normalized_import_file": _normalize_match_path(recorded_file),
    93→        "packet_path": str(provenance.get("packet_path", "")).strip(),
    94→        "normalized_packet_path": _normalize_match_path(provenance.get("packet_path")),
    95→        "packet_sha256": str(provenance.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip(),
    96→        "runner": str(provenance.get("runner", "")).strip(),
    97→        "assessment_dimensions": sorted(
    98→            key.strip()
    99→            for key in assessments.keys()
   100→            if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   101→        ),
   102→        "issue_count": len(issues),
   103→    }
   104→
   105→
   106→def pending_import_scores_meta(
   107→    plan: PlanModel,
   108→    state: StateModel,
   109→) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   110→    """Return normalized pending score-import metadata, if any."""
   111→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   112→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   113→    meta = refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   114→    if isinstance(meta, dict) and meta:
   115→        return meta
   116→    issues_only_audit = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   117→    if issues_only_audit is None:
   118→        return None
   119→    return _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   120→        import_file=str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")),
   121→        import_payload=None,
   122→        issues_only_audit=issues_only_audit,
   123→    )
   124→
   125→
   126→def import_scores_meta_matches(
   127→    meta: dict[str, Any] | None,
   128→    *,
   129→    import_file: str,
   130→    import_payload: dict[str, Any],
   131→) -> tuple[bool, list[str]]:
   132→    """Return whether the current import matches the pending score-import batch."""
   133→    if not isinstance(meta, dict) or not meta:
   134→        return True, []
   135→
   136→    mismatches: list[str] = []
   137→    expected_file = str(meta.get("normalized_import_file", "")).strip()
   138→    current_file = _normalize_match_path(import_file) or ""
   139→    if expected_file and current_file != expected_file:
   140→        mismatches.append(
   141→            f"expected import file {meta.get('import_file')}, got {import_file}"
   142→        )
   143→
   144→    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   145→    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
   146→    expected_hash = str(meta.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   147→    current_hash = str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   148→    if expected_hash and current_hash != expected_hash:
   149→        mismatches.append(
   150→            f"expected packet_sha256 {expected_hash}, got {current_hash or '<missing>'}"
   151→        )
   152→
   153→    expected_packet_path = str(meta.get("normalized_packet_path", "")).strip()
   154→    current_packet_path = _normalize_match_path(provenance_dict.get("packet_path")) or ""
   155→    if expected_packet_path and current_packet_path != expected_packet_path:
   156→        mismatches.append(
   157→            "expected packet_path "
   158→            f"{meta.get('packet_path')}, got {provenance_dict.get('packet_path') or '<missing>'}"
   159→        )
   160→
   161→    expected_dims = meta.get("assessment_dimensions") or []
   162→    if expected_dims:
   163→        assessments = import_payload.get("assessments")
   164→        assessment_keys = sorted(
   165→            key.strip()
   166→            for key in (assessments if isinstance(assessments, dict) else {}).keys()
   167→            if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   168→        )
   169→        if assessment_keys != expected_dims:
   170→            mismatches.append(
   171→                f"expected assessment dimensions {expected_dims}, got {assessment_keys}"
   172→            )
   173→    return not mismatches, mismatches
   174→
   175→
   176→def _clear_pending_import_scores(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   177→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   178→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   179→        order[:] = [item for item in order if item != WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]
   180→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   181→    refresh_state.pop(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY, None)
   182→
   183→
   184→def _no_unscored(
   185→    state: StateModel,
   186→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
   187→) -> bool:
   188→    """Return True when no unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain."""
   189→    if policy is not None:
   190→        return not policy.unscored_ids
   191→    return not stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
   192→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   193→    )
   194→
   195→
   196→def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
   197→    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
   198→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   199→    if item_id not in order:
   200→        order.append(item_id)
```

```
1→"""Plan sync helpers for review-import flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import (
    10→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    11→    compute_open_issue_ids,
    12→    sync_triage_needed,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
    19→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
    20→
    21→    new_ids: set[str]
    22→    added_to_queue: list[str]
    23→    triage_injected: bool
    24→    stale_pruned_from_queue: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    25→    triage_injected_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    26→    triage_deferred: bool = False
    27→
    28→
    29→def _has_triage_baseline(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    30→    """Return True when triage has recorded at least one baseline issue ID."""
    31→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    32→    triaged_ids = meta.get("triaged_ids", [])
    33→    return bool(triaged_ids)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
    37→    plan: PlanModel,
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    41→) -> set[str]:
    42→    """Return review IDs that should be synced into queue_order after import.
    43→
    44→    No triage baseline yet: include all currently-open review IDs so the
    45→    first import cannot drop follow-up work.
    46→    Existing triage baseline: include only IDs that are new since triage.
    47→    """
    48→    if _has_triage_baseline(plan):
    49→        return compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
    50→    return set(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else compute_open_issue_ids(state)
    51→
    52→
    53→def _is_review_queue_id(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True for queue IDs representing review/concerns issues."""
    55→    return issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::")
    56→
    57→
    58→def _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    *,
    61→    live_open_review_ids: set[str],
    62→) -> list[str]:
    63→    """Remove stale review IDs from queue-related plan containers.
    64→
    65→    Review imports can auto-resolve prior review IDs. These IDs must be removed
    66→    from queue_order (and linked queue metadata) or the queue can drift from
    67→    state reality.
    68→    """
    69→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    70→    stale_ids = sorted(
    71→        {
    72→            issue_id
    73→            for issue_id in order
    74→            if _is_review_queue_id(issue_id) and issue_id not in live_open_review_ids
    75→        }
    76→    )
    77→    if not stale_ids:
    78→        return []
    79→
    80→    stale_set = set(stale_ids)
    81→    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in stale_set]
    82→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, stale_set)
    83→
    84→    deferred = plan.get("deferred")
    85→    if isinstance(deferred, list):
    86→        plan["deferred"] = [issue_id for issue_id in deferred if issue_id not in stale_set]
    87→
    88→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
    89→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
    90→        for issue_id in stale_set:
    91→            skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
    92→
    93→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    94→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
    95→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
    96→            continue
    97→        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
    98→            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in stale_set
    99→        ]
   100→
```
