```
1→"""Lifecycle visibility filtering for work-queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.plan_queue import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
     7→
     8→
     9→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    10→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue.
    11→
    12→    Checks both individual issues and collapsed clusters — clusters
    13→    contain objective issues grouped by the queue builder.
    14→    """
    15→    return any(
    16→        item.get("kind") in ("issue", "cluster")
    17→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    18→        for item in items
    19→    )
    20→
    21→
    22→def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    23→    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
    24→    return any(
    25→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    26→        and item.get("initial_review")
    27→        for item in items
    28→    )
    29→
    30→
    31→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    32→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
    33→    if item.get("detector") == "subjective_review":
    34→        return True
    35→    return (
    36→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    37→        and not item.get("initial_review")
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→def _has_endgame_subjective(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    42→    """True if any non-initial subjective review items are pending."""
    43→    return any(_is_endgame_only(item) for item in items)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _has_triage_stages(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    47→    """True if any pending triage stage items are in the queue."""
    48→    return any(
    49→        item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
    50→        and str(item.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
    51→        for item in items
    52→    )
    53→
    54→
    55→def _is_triage_stage(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    56→    """True when item is a triage workflow stage."""
    57→    return (
    58→        item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
    59→        and str(item.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→
    63→def _is_force_visible(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    64→    """True when the item is explicitly escalated past objective gating."""
    65→    return bool(item.get("force_visible"))
    66→
    67→
    68→def apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    69→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules."""
    70→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
    71→        return [
    72→            item for item in items
    73→            if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review")
    74→        ]
    75→    # Enforce fixed endgame phase order:
    76→    #   subjective review -> score workflow -> triage
    77→    # When non-initial subjective reruns are pending and objective work is
    78→    # already drained, keep focus on subjective reruns and defer workflow/triage.
    79→    if _has_endgame_subjective(items) and not _has_objective_items(items):
    80→        return [
    81→            item for item in items
    82→            if _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item)
    83→        ]
    84→    if _has_triage_stages(items):
    85→        # Triage should not block while objective queue work still exists.
    86→        if _has_objective_items(items):
    87→            return [
    88→                item for item in items
    89→                if (
    90→                    (not _is_triage_stage(item) or _is_force_visible(item))
    91→                    and (not _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item))
    92→                )
    93→            ]
    94→        return [
    95→            item for item in items
    96→            if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    97→        ]
    98→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
    99→        return items
   100→    return [item for item in items if not _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item)]
   101→
   102→
   103→__all__ = ["apply_lifecycle_filter"]
   104→
```
```

1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.config import (
    14→    DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    15→    target_strict_score_from_config,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    18→from desloppify.engine.plan_queue import (
    19→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    20→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    21→    has_living_plan,
    22→    load_plan,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    25→
    26→
    27→class _PlanAutoLoad:
    28→    """Sentinel type: auto-load plan from disk."""
    29→
    30→
    31→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    32→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = _PlanAutoLoad()
    33→PlanOption = dict | None | _PlanAutoLoad
    34→
    35→
    36→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    37→class PlanLoadStatus:
    38→    """Resolved plan load result with degraded-mode signaling."""
    39→
    40→    plan: dict | None
    41→    degraded: bool
    42→    error_kind: str | None = None
    43→
    44→
    45→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    46→class QueueContext:
    47→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    48→
    49→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through
    50→    ``build_work_queue``, ``plan_aware_queue_breakdown``, and command
    51→    helpers so every call site agrees on plan, target, and policy.
    52→    """
    53→
    54→    plan: dict | None
    55→    target_strict: float
    56→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility
    57→    plan_load_status: PlanLoadStatus
    58→
    59→
    60→def resolve_plan_load_status(
    61→    *,
    62→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    63→) -> PlanLoadStatus:
    64→    """Resolve plan loading and explicitly report degraded mode."""
    65→    if not isinstance(plan, _PlanAutoLoad):
    66→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    67→
    68→    if not has_living_plan():
    69→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=None, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    70→
    71→    try:
    72→        resolved_plan = load_plan()
    73→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    74→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    75→            plan=None,
    76→            degraded=True,
    77→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    78→        )
    79→    return PlanLoadStatus(plan=resolved_plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    80→
    81→
    82→def queue_context(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    config: dict | None = None,
    86→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    87→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    88→) -> QueueContext:
    89→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    90→
    91→    Resolution order:
    92→
    93→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    94→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    95→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
    96→       via ``target_strict_score_from_config``; final fallback is ``95.0``.
    97→    3. **policy** — ``compute_subjective_visibility(state, ...)`` with the
    98→       resolved plan and target_strict so every downstream consumer sees
    99→       the same objective-vs-subjective balance.
   100→    """
   101→    # --- resolve plan ---
   102→    plan_load_status = resolve_plan_load_status(plan=plan)
   103→    resolved_plan = plan_load_status.plan
   104→
   105→    # --- resolve target_strict ---
   106→    if target_strict is not None:
   107→        resolved_target = target_strict
   108→    elif config is not None:
   109→        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   110→    else:
   111→        resolved_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   112→
   113→    # --- resolve policy ---
   114→    resolved_policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   115→        state,
   116→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   117→        plan=resolved_plan,
   118→    )
   119→
   120→    return QueueContext(
   121→        plan=resolved_plan,
   122→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   123→        policy=resolved_policy,
   124→        plan_load_status=plan_load_status,
   125→    )
   126→
   127→
   128→__all__ = [
   129→    "PlanLoadStatus",
   130→    "QueueContext",
   131→    "queue_context",
   132→    "resolve_plan_load_status",
   133→]
   134→
```

```
1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
    14→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→
    19→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    20→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    21→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    22→})
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    27→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    28→
    29→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    30→    objective_count: int  # how many
    31→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    32→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    33→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    34→
    35→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    36→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    37→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    38→            return True  # unconditional
    39→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    40→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    41→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    42→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    43→        return False
    44→
    45→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    46→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    47→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    48→            return False  # never evict unscored
    49→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    50→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    51→        return False
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    55→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    56→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    57→
    58→
    59→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    61→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    62→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    63→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    64→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    65→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    66→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    67→            return True
    68→    return False
    69→
    70→
    71→class _ScanPathFromStatePolicy:
    72→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    73→
    74→
    75→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = _ScanPathFromStatePolicy()
    76→ScanPathPolicyOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromStatePolicy
    77→
    78→
    79→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    83→    scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    84→    plan: dict | None = None,
    85→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    86→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    87→
    88→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    89→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    90→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    91→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    92→
    93→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    94→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import (
    97→        current_stale_ids,
    98→        current_under_target_ids,
    99→        current_unscored_ids,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   103→        state.get("scan_path")
   104→        if isinstance(scan_path, _ScanPathFromStatePolicy)
   105→        else scan_path
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   109→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   110→
   111→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   112→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   113→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   114→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   115→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   116→    objective_count = sum(
   117→        1
   118→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   119→        if issue.get("status") == Status.OPEN
   120→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   121→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   122→        and not _is_evidence_only(issue)
   123→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(issue.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   124→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   128→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   129→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   132→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   133→        objective_count=objective_count,
   134→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   135→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   136→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   142→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   143→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   144→]
   145→
```

```
     2→
```

```
    20→    save_plan,
    21→)

    32→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Set augmented description on issues."""
    34→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    35→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    36→        return
    37→
    38→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    39→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
    40→
    41→    plan = load_plan()
    42→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    43→    if not issue_ids:
    44→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
    45→        return
    46→
    47→    for fid in issue_ids:
    48→        describe_issue(plan, fid, text or None)
    49→    append_log_entry(plan, "describe", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user", detail={"text": text or None})
    50→    save_plan(plan)
    51→    print(colorize(f"  Set description on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
    52→
    53→
    54→def cmd_plan_note(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Set note on issues."""
    56→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    57→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    58→        return
    59→
    60→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    61→    text: str | None = getattr(args, "text", None)
    62→
    63→    plan = load_plan()
    64→    issue_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    65→    if not issue_ids:
    66→        print(colorize("  No matching issues found.", "yellow"))
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    for fid in issue_ids:
    70→        annotate_issue(plan, fid, text)
    71→    append_log_entry(plan, "note", issue_ids=issue_ids, actor="user", note=text)
    72→    save_plan(plan)
    73→    print(colorize(f"  Set note on {len(issue_ids)} issue(s).", "green"))
    74→
    75→
    76→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    77→    """Reopen resolved issues from plan context."""
    78→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    79→
    80→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
    81→    state_file = (
    82→        raw_state_path
    83→        if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path)
    84→        else Path(raw_state_path)
    85→        if raw_state_path
    86→        else None
    87→    )
    88→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    89→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
    90→
    91→    reopened: list[str] = []
    92→    for pattern in patterns:
    93→        reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_issues(state_data, pattern, "open"))
    94→
    95→    if not reopened:
    96→        print(colorize("  No resolved issues matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
    97→        return
    98→
    99→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   100→    purge_uncommitted_ids(plan, reopened)
   101→
   102→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   103→    count = 0
   104→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   105→    for fid in reopened:
   106→        if fid in skipped:
   107→            skipped.pop(fid)
   108→            count += 1
   109→        if fid not in order:
   110→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   111→            order.add(fid)
   112→            count += 1
   113→
   114→    append_log_entry(plan, "reopen", issue_ids=reopened, actor="user")
   115→    clear_postflight_scan_completion(plan, issue_ids=reopened)
   116→    save_plan_state_transactional(
   117→        plan=plan,
   118→        plan_path=plan_file,
   119→        state_data=state_data,
   120→        state_path_value=state_file,
   121→    )
   122→
   123→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} issue(s).", "green"))
   124→    if count:
   125→        print(colorize("  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   126→
   127→

   130→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)

   141→
   142→    if not cluster_name:

   147→            print("  No active focus.")
```

```
1→"""I/O and transactional persistence helpers for plan override commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from pathlib import Path
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
     8→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import CommandError
     9→from desloppify.engine.plan_state import (
    10→    PlanModel,
    11→    get_plan_file,
    12→    plan_path_for_state,
    13→    save_plan,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel, get_state_file, save_state
    16→
    17→
    18→def _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    19→    return path if path is not None else get_state_file()
    20→
    21→
    22→def _resolve_plan_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    23→    return path if path is not None else get_plan_file()
    24→
    25→
    26→def _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    27→    if state_file is None:
    28→        return None
    29→    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    30→
    31→
    32→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    33→    if not path.exists():
    34→        return None
    35→    return path.read_text()
    36→
    37→
    38→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    39→    if snapshot is None:
    40→        try:
    41→            path.unlink()
    42→        except FileNotFoundError:
    43→            return
    44→        return
    45→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _rollback_snapshots(
    49→    *,
    50→    state_path: Path,
    51→    state_snapshot: str | None,
    52→    plan_path: Path,
    53→    plan_snapshot: str | None,
    54→) -> list[str]:
    55→    failed_paths: list[str] = []
    56→    for path, snapshot in (
    57→        (state_path, state_snapshot),
    58→        (plan_path, plan_snapshot),
    59→    ):
    60→        try:
    61→            _restore_file_snapshot(path, snapshot)
    62→        except OSError:
    63→            failed_paths.append(str(path))
    64→    return failed_paths
    65→
    66→
    67→def save_plan_state_transactional(
    68→    *,
    69→    plan: PlanModel,
    70→    plan_path: Path | None,
    71→    state_data: StateModel,
    72→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    73→) -> None:
    74→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    75→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    76→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    77→    try:
    78→        plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    79→        state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    80→    except OSError as exc:
    81→        raise CommandError(f"could not snapshot plan/state before save: {exc}") from exc
    82→
    83→    try:
    84→        save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
    85→        save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
    86→    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as exc:
    87→        failed_paths = _rollback_snapshots(
    88→            state_path=effective_state_path,
    89→            state_snapshot=state_snapshot,
    90→            plan_path=effective_plan_path,
    91→            plan_snapshot=plan_snapshot,
    92→        )
    93→        rollback_note = ""
    94→        if failed_paths:
    95→            rollback_note = (
    96→                "; rollback may be incomplete for: "
    97→                + ", ".join(failed_paths)
    98→            )
    99→        raise CommandError(
   100→            f"could not save plan/state transaction: {exc}{rollback_note}"
   101→        ) from exc
   102→
   103→
   104→__all__ = [
   105→    "_plan_file_for_state",
   106→    "save_plan_state_transactional",
   107→]
   108→
```

## Investigation Report: Review-Related Lifecycle Rules

I've completed a thorough investigation of the codebase. **The review-related lifecycle logic IS coherent but split across multiple specialized modules, with different representations used at different layers.** Here's what I found:

---

### 1. **Top-Level Lifecycle Gate** (`lifecycle.py`)

The **single, centralized gating logic** lives in:  
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/lifecycle.py`

**Function:** `apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]`

**Rules (in order of precedence):**
1. **Initial review phase:** If any `subjective_dimension` items with `initial_review=True` exist → show ONLY those
2. **Endgame subjective phase:** If non-initial subjective items exist AND objective backlog is empty → show those + force-visible items
3. **Triage phase:** If triage stages exist AND objective backlog still exists → hide triage/endgame items (unless force-visible)
4. **Final fallback:** If objective backlog exists → hide all endgame-only items (unless force-visible)

**Key decision helpers:**
- `_has_objective_items()` — checks if non-`NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` issues exist
- `_is_endgame_only()` — true for `detector=="subjective_review"` OR non-initial subjective dimensions
- `_has_initial_reviews()` — true if any unassessed dimensions need scoring
- `_has_endgame_subjective()` — true if re-review items are pending

---

### 2. **Definition of Non-Objective Work** (`subjective.py`)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/policy/subjective.py`

**Constant (line 20-22):**
```python
NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
})
```

These 4 detectors define the boundary between objective mechanical work and subjective review work:
- `review` — human-written findings for a specific dimension
- `concerns` — holistic/structural concerns (like architectural issues)
- `subjective_review` — synthesized signals from review completion
- `subjective_assessment` — computed work items for dimensions below target

**Related policy class:** `SubjectiveVisibility` (frozen dataclass) consolidates:
- `has_objective_backlog` (computed from NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS exclusion)
- `objective_count` (count of open non-subjective issues)
- `unscored_ids` (initial-review dimensions)
- `stale_ids` (re-review dimensions)

---

### 3. **Synthetic Item Builders** (Two-layer representation)

#### Layer 1: Subjective Dimensions
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic.py`  
**Function:** `build_subjective_items()` (lines 181-274)

Builds `kind="subjective_dimension"` items:
- **ID pattern:** `f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}"`
- **Detector:** `"subjective_assessment"`
- **Key metadata:**
  - `initial_review` (bool) — is it unassessed/placeholder?
  - `stale` (bool) — does it need re-review?
  - `open_review_issues` (int) — how many `detector=="review"` issues exist?

#### Layer 2: Review Issues (Imported)
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/review/__init__.py`  
(Wrapper to `/intelligence/review/importing/per_file.py` and `/holistic.py`)

Imported review issues have:
- **Detector:** `"review"` (per-dimension findings) or `"concerns"` (holistic)
- **ID pattern:** `review::path::dim::identifier` or `concerns::type::reason`
- **Detail field:** includes `dimension` key for mapping to dimension
- **Status:** "open" or resolved

---

### 4. **Workflow Lifecycle Sync** (`workflow.py`)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py`

Controls injection/removal of workflow items based on subjective state:

| Workflow Item | Injection Condition |
|---|---|
| `workflow::score-checkpoint` | All initial dims scored → checkpoint score |
| `workflow::create-plan` | All dims scored + objective backlog exists |
| `workflow::import-scores` | After issues-only import (scores deferred) |
| `workflow::communicate-score` | Dims complete + no mid-cycle sentinel |

Uses `SubjectiveVisibility` policy for decisions.

---

### 5. **Review Import Plan Reconciliation** (`reconcile_review_import.py`)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile_review_import.py`

Handles ID synchronization after review imports:
- **Helper:** `_is_review_queue_id(id)` checks for `review::*` or `concerns::*` prefixes
- **Stale removal:** Auto-resolved review IDs pruned from queue, clusters, skipped entries
- **Triage baseline:** If triage already has triaged_ids, only add new review IDs; otherwise include all open

---

### 6. **Constants and Prefixes** (`constants.py`)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/constants.py`

```python
SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
```

These prefixes distinguish synthetic items from regular issues.

---

### 7. **Integrity Helpers** (`integrity.py`)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/intelligence/integrity.py`

```python
def is_subjective_review_open(issue: dict) -> bool:
    """Return True when issue is an open subjective-review signal."""
    return (
        issue.get("status") == "open"
        and issue.get("detector") == "subjective_review"
    )

def subjective_review_open_breakdown(issues) -> (total, reason_counts, holistic_counts):
    """Count open subjective_review issues by reason."""
```

Used for reporting and preflight checks.

---

### 8. **Test Integration**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_lifecycle_transitions.py`

Full lifecycle tests walk through:
1. Fresh scan → placeholder dimensions + no objective work
2. Initial reviews → only `subjective_dimension` items visible
3. Reviews complete → inject `workflow::score-checkpoint` → `workflow::communicate-score`
4. Objective queue drained → endgame subjective reruns (stale dims) visible
5. All subjective complete → create plan + triage stages

---

## Summary: Where Is Lifecycle Logic Expressed?

| Aspect | Primary Location | Secondary |
|---|---|---|
| Visibility gating | `lifecycle.py::apply_lifecycle_filter()` | `synthetic.py`, `workflow.py` |
| Detector classification | `subjective.py::NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` | `integrity.py::is_subjective_review_open()` |
| Item construction | `synthetic.py::build_subjective_items()` | `synthetic_workflow.py` for workflow items |
| Dimension scoring | `synthetic.py::subjective_strict_scores()` | state integration in `state.py` |
| Review import sync | `reconcile_review_import.py` | state merge in `merge_issues.py` |
| Constants | `constants.py` (SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES) | None |

**Verdict:** The lifecycle logic is **coherent and unified at the gate level** (`lifecycle.py`), but **expressed across multiple module boundaries** because:
- Policy decisions (what counts as objective) live in `subjective.py`
- Item construction (how to represent work) lives in `synthetic.py` and `synthetic_workflow.py`
- Import sync (how to track review status changes) lives in `reconcile_review_import.py`
- Integrity checks (operational helpers) live in `integrity.py`

This is a **good separation of concerns** — each module has a clear responsibility. The single gate (`lifecycle.py`) then composes these pieces coherently.

## Investigation Report: Dual-Source Queue Behavior and Synthetic Workflow Identity Issues

I have completed a thorough investigation of the work queue assembly architecture in desloppify. Here are my findings:

### 1. **Queue Assembly: Multiple Independent Sources**

The work queue is built from **three independent parallel sources**:

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 114-120

```python
items = build_issue_items(
    state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
    scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
)
items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
```

- **Source 1: State-driven issues** — `build_issue_items()` reads directly from `state["issues"]` (mechanical findings)
- **Source 2: Subjective dimensions** — Computed on-the-fly from `state["dimension_scores"]` (synthetic dimensions)
- **Source 3: Plan-driven workflow** — Generated from `plan["queue_order"]` (triage stages, checkpoints, create-plan)

### 2. **Synthetic Workflow Items Lack Both Identity and Provenance**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/constants.py` lines 26-45

Workflow item IDs are **bare string constants** with no backward reference to any backing data:

```python
WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID = "workflow::deferred-disposition"
```

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py` lines 115-171

The item builders check for these IDs in `plan["queue_order"]`, then construct a full `WorkQueueItem` dict **on-the-fly** with computed data:

```python
def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
        return None
    # ... compute snapshot from state and plan ...
    return {
        "id": WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
        "tier": 1,
        "confidence": "high",
        # ... rest of the dict
    }
```

**Critical flaw:** The item exists **only during the queue build**, computed from `state["dimension_scores"]` and `plan["plan_start_scores"]`. It has **no persistent storage**, no versioning, no change audit trail.

### 3. **Plan-Driven vs. State-Driven Duality**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` lines 206-226

```python
def _gather_workflow_items(
    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
        return []
    
    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
    for builder in (
        build_score_checkpoint_item,
        build_import_scores_item,
        build_communicate_score_item,
    ):
        item = builder(plan, state)
        if item is not None:
            items.append(item)
    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
    # ...
```

The builders check **only** `plan["queue_order"]` to decide whether to build items. Once an ID is in the queue order, the builders materialize the item from current state/plan data. **This creates a split responsibility:**

- **Plan**: Controls *whether* an item appears (membership in `queue_order`)
- **State + Plan**: Provides *what* the item contains (computed at build time)

### 4. **Visibility Policy: Independent from Queue Identity**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/policy/subjective.py` lines 25-57

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SubjectiveVisibility:
    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    
    has_objective_backlog: bool
    objective_count: int
    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]
    stale_ids: frozenset[str]
    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]
    
    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
            return True  # unconditional
        if fid in self.stale_ids:
            return not self.has_objective_backlog
        # ...
```

This policy is computed **once per operation** but NOT tied to the queue item builders. It's a separate decision tree controlling *which subjective IDs should be in the plan*, but the workflow builders ignore it entirely.

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/lifecycle.py` lines 68-100

The lifecycle filter applies **separate rules** to decide visibility:

```python
def apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules."""
    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
        return [item for item in items if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" ...]
    if _has_endgame_subjective(items) and not _has_objective_items(items):
        return [item for item in items if _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item)]
    # ...
```

None of this visibility logic touches synthetic workflow items. They use `force_visible` but it's set at item construction time (never), not computed dynamically.

### 5. **Queue Order is the Sole Controller, But No Audit Trail**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py` lines 196-205

```python
def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
    order = plan["queue_order"]
    if item_id not in order:
        order.append(item_id)
    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
        skipped.pop(item_id, None)
    return QueueSyncResult(injected=[item_id])
```

- IDs are appended to `queue_order` when sync functions detect conditions
- No persistent record of *why* the item was added
- No link to the state/plan data that triggered the injection
- Clearing happens via `_clear_pending_import_scores()` (line 176) which modifies `queue_order` directly

**Key insight:** The plan stores string IDs, but these IDs have **no fields or metadata** beyond being a list of strings. The actual item content is materialized at display time from current state/plan values.

### 6. **Inconsistency Risk: Plan and State Can Drift**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py` lines 267-325

The `sync_import_scores_needed()` function shows the duality:

```python
def sync_import_scores_needed(...) -> QueueSyncResult:
    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import."""
    # ...
    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
        if latest_issues_only is None:
            stale_pending = True  # No recent issues-only in audit trail
    if stale_pending:
        _clear_pending_import_scores(plan)
        return QueueSyncResult(pruned=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
    
    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
        if assessment_mode == "issues_only":
            refresh_state[_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY] = ...  # Update metadata
            return QueueSyncResult(resurfaced=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
    # ... inject if conditions met
```

The condition for removal is: "Item is in queue AND no issues-only audit entry exists". But the audit trail is in `state["assessment_import_audit"]`, not in the plan. **If state gets corrupted or rolled back, the plan becomes orphaned.**

### 7. **No Canonical Source of Truth**

The queue builder has three entry points:

1. **Mechanical issues** — from `state["issues"]` (canonical, versioned, persisted)
2. **Subjective items** — computed from `state["dimension_scores"]` + visibility policy (rule-based, stateless)
3. **Workflow items** — from `plan["queue_order"]` IDs, materialized from current state/plan (volatile, reconstructed)

None of these source are reconciled:
- The plan can have a workflow ID that no longer makes sense in current state
- State can delete dimension scores, making subjective items impossible to reconstruct
- Issue data can change, affecting impact scoring and lifecycle filtering

---

## Summary

**The dual-source behavior is:** Queue contains plan-controlled IDs (workflow items) whose content is state-derived at build time, plus state-driven issues (mechanical + subjective). These sources aren't unified.

**Synthetic workflow identity problems:**
1. No persistent metadata/versioning for workflow items
2. Identity is bare string constant; provenance computed on-the-fly
3. Lifecycle/visibility rules applied independently, not integrated with synthetic item construction
4. No audit trail linking workflow ID presence in `queue_order` to the state conditions that triggered injection
5. Stale/orphaned items possible when state changes without plan sync

**Files to review for architecture improvements:**
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` — queue assembly
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py` — item builders (no metadata)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py` — sync logic (missing audit trail)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/lifecycle.py` — visibility (disconnected from item construction)

## Investigation Summary: Global State Coupling in Mutation Paths

Based on my thorough code analysis, here are my findings on the "hidden coupling to global state" claims:

### 1. **How `has_living_plan()` and `load_plan()` Work**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/persistence.py` (lines 37-183)

- **`load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel`** (line 81)
  - Defaults to `_default_plan_file()` when `path` is None
  - `_default_plan_file()` resolves to `get_plan_file()` which returns `get_state_dir() / "plan.json"`
  - `get_state_dir()` returns `get_project_root() / ".desloppify"` (from `engine/_state/schema.py`)

- **`has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool`** (line 162)
  - Defaults to `_default_plan_file()` when `path` is None
  - Returns True only if plan exists AND has user intent (queue_order, overrides, or clusters)

- **`save_plan(plan, path: Path | None = None) -> None`** (line 132)
  - Also defaults to `_default_plan_file()` when `path` is None
  - Supports atomic writes with `.json.bak` backup

### 2. **`plan_path_for_state()` Analysis**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/persistence.py` (line 157)

```python
def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
```

**When was it introduced?** Commit `6268a61` (2026-02-27): "Remove top-level resolve command, consolidate into plan subcommands"

This is a **pure function** that takes a state path and derives the plan path from it. It's NOT new—it was added alongside the refactoring to consolidate resolve subcommands into plan subcommands.

### 3. **`sync_plan_after_import` Analysis**

**Current version (with local changes):**
```python
def sync_plan_after_import(
    state: dict,
    diff: dict,
    assessment_mode: str,
    *,
    state_file: str | Path | None = None,  # NEW on current branch
    config: dict | None = None,
    import_file: str | None = None,
    import_payload: dict | None = None,
) -> None:
```

**At commit HEAD:**
```python
def sync_plan_after_import(
    state: dict,
    diff: dict,
    assessment_mode: str,
    *,
    config: dict | None = None,
) -> None:
```

**Changes on current branch (0.9.5):**
- Added `state_file` parameter to function signature (line 71)
- Now uses `plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))` instead of implicit defaults (lines 79-80)
- Passes explicit `plan_path` to all `has_living_plan()` and `load_plan()` calls
- Saves with explicit path: `plan_queue_mod.save_plan(plan, plan_path)` (line 233)

This is a **recent addition** fixing the global state coupling issue.

### 4. **Caller Analysis: Explicit vs Implicit Paths**

**load_plan() calls:**
- Total: 67 non-test calls
- **With explicit path:** 9 calls (13%)
  - `override_misc.py:99` - `load_plan(plan_file)`
  - `override_skip.py` - 2 calls with `plan_file`
  - `scan/plan_reconcile.py` - `load_plan(plan_path)`
  - `exclude.py` - `load_plan(plan_file)`
  - `review/importing/cmd.py` - `load_plan(plan_path)` (NEW)
  - `review/importing/plan_sync.py` - `load_plan(plan_path)` (NEW on 0.9.5)
  - `persistence.py` - internal call in `has_living_plan()`
- **With default (implicit):** 58 calls (87%)
  - Most in `app/commands/plan/` subcommands (cluster ops, triage, queue rendering, etc.)

**has_living_plan() calls:**
- Total: 4 production calls
- **With explicit path:** 2 calls (50%)
  - `review/importing/cmd.py` - `has_living_plan(plan_path)`
  - `review/importing/plan_sync.py` - `has_living_plan(plan_path)`
- **With default (implicit):** 2 calls (50%)
  - `resolve/living_plan.py` - `has_living_plan()`
  - `scan/reporting/integrity_report.py` - `has_living_plan()`
  - `_work_queue/context.py` - `has_living_plan()`

**save_plan() calls:**
- Total: 48 non-test calls
- **With explicit path:** 5 calls (10%)
  - `override_io.py` - `save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)`
  - `override_skip.py` - 2 calls with `plan_file`
  - `scan/plan_reconcile.py` - `save_plan(plan, plan_path)`
  - `exclude.py` - `save_plan(plan, plan_file)`
  - `review/importing/plan_sync.py` - `save_plan(plan, plan_path)` (NEW on 0.9.5)
- **With default (implicit):** 43 calls (90%)
  - Most in `app/commands/plan/` subcommands

### 5. **Verdict: Real Pattern, Recently Being Fixed**

**CONFIRMED: There IS a significant pattern of implicit global state dependency in mutation paths.**

- **58 out of 67 `load_plan()` calls** (87%) rely on implicit CWD-based defaults
- **43 out of 48 `save_plan()` calls** (90%) rely on implicit CWD-based defaults
- **On the current branch (0.9.5), the import reconciliation path is being explicitly fixed** by adding `state_file` plumbing and deriving plan paths from state paths

**The coupling chain is:**
```
load_plan() [no path arg]
  → _default_plan_file()
  → get_state_dir()
  → get_project_root() / ".desloppify"
  → Result: hardcoded to current working directory's .desloppify/plan.json
```

**Current branch changes show conscious effort to break this coupling** for review import workflows by:
1. Adding explicit `state_file` parameter to `sync_plan_after_import`
2. Deriving plan paths from state file location using `plan_path_for_state()`
3. Passing explicit paths through the mutation call chain

However, the vast majority of plan commands (87%+ of load_plan, 90%+ of save_plan) still rely on implicit defaults.

> DEVELOPER

I need to design an implementation plan for improving the lifecycle/visibility architecture in a Python CLI tool called desloppify. Here's the context from my investigation:

## Current Architecture

### The Problem
There are multiple "review-related" work types that need consistent lifecycle treatment:
- `subjective::*` — dimension prompt items (detector: `subjective_assessment`)
- `subjective_review::*` — file-level review issues (detector: `subjective_review`)  
- `review::*` — imported per-dimension findings (detector: `review`)
- `concerns::*` — imported holistic concerns (detector: `concerns`)
- `workflow::*` — synthetic workflow actions (detector: `workflow`)

### Where lifecycle logic lives today

1. **`engine/_plan/policy/subjective.py`** — `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS = frozenset({"review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment"})` — this is the canonical list of non-objective detectors

2. **`engine/_work_queue/lifecycle.py`** — `apply_lifecycle_filter()` — the single visibility gate. It has helpers:
   - `_is_endgame_only(item)` — checks `detector == "subjective_review"` OR `kind == "subjective_dimension" and not initial_review`
   - `_has_objective_items(items)` — checks for items NOT in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS (imports it)
   - `_has_initial_reviews(items)` — checks for initial subjective dimensions
   - `_has_endgame_subjective(items)` — checks for non-initial subjective dimensions

3. **`engine/_plan/sync/workflow.py`** — controls injection/removal of workflow items into plan queue_order

4. **`engine/_work_queue/core.py`** — assembles the queue from 3 independent sources:
   - `build_issue_items()` from state["issues"]
   - `_gather_subjective_items()` computed from state dimension scores
   - `_gather_workflow_items()` from plan queue_order

5. **`engine/_plan/constants.py`** — `SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES`, workflow ID constants

### Bugs that motivated this investigation
- `subjective_review` detector items weren't treated as endgame-only (2-line fix in lifecycle.py)
- `unclustered_review_issues()` helper didn't exclude skipped issues
- `workflow::import-scores` wasn't bound to a specific batch (could satisfy with wrong file)
- `sync_plan_after_import` used implicit CWD plan path instead of deriving from state_file
- 87% of load_plan() calls and 90% of save_plan() calls use implicit CWD defaults

### What the user wants
1. One explicit lifecycle model for all review-related work types
2. No cwd/global-plan fallbacks in mutation paths
3. Batch-bound workflow items with strong provenance
4. Queue construction driven by canonical source of truth with uniform visibility policy

### Constraints
- 4960 tests passing, can't break them
- This is a CLI tool (not a library), so some ambient state is acceptable for read-only display paths
- The user's CLAUDE.md says "no backward compat for import paths/APIs" so internal refactoring is fine
- Need to be practical — this is a working system, not a rewrite

## Design Questions

1. Should `_is_endgame_only()` derive its answer from `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` instead of hardcoding detector names? That would make it automatically correct for any detector in the set.

2. Should workflow items carry a `WorkflowItemMeta` dataclass that persists alongside their queue_order entry, rather than storing metadata in `refresh_state`?

3. Should mutation paths (import, scan reconcile, triage) require an explicit `PlanContext` object that encapsulates the plan path, rather than allowing implicit defaults?

4. Is there value in a `QueueItemClassification` enum or similar that maps every item type to its lifecycle phase, rather than having lifecycle.py infer it from combinations of fields?

Please design a practical, incremental plan. Focus on the changes that would have prevented the bugs discovered, not on theoretical purity. Consider the tradeoffs of each change.

> AGENT

I'll explore the codebase systematically to understand the current architecture before designing the plan.

```
1→"""Lifecycle visibility filtering for work-queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine.plan_queue import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
     6→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
     7→
     8→
     9→def _has_objective_items(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    10→    """True if any objective mechanical work items remain in the queue.
    11→
    12→    Checks both individual issues and collapsed clusters — clusters
    13→    contain objective issues grouped by the queue builder.
    14→    """
    15→    return any(
    16→        item.get("kind") in ("issue", "cluster")
    17→        and item.get("detector", "") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    18→        for item in items
    19→    )
    20→
    21→
    22→def _has_initial_reviews(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    23→    """True if any unassessed subjective dimensions need initial review."""
    24→    return any(
    25→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    26→        and item.get("initial_review")
    27→        for item in items
    28→    )
    29→
    30→
    31→def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    32→    """True if this item should only appear when the objective queue is drained."""
    33→    if item.get("detector") == "subjective_review":
    34→        return True
    35→    return (
    36→        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
    37→        and not item.get("initial_review")
    38→    )
    39→
    40→
    41→def _has_endgame_subjective(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    42→    """True if any non-initial subjective review items are pending."""
    43→    return any(_is_endgame_only(item) for item in items)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _has_triage_stages(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> bool:
    47→    """True if any pending triage stage items are in the queue."""
    48→    return any(
    49→        item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
    50→        and str(item.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
    51→        for item in items
    52→    )
    53→
    54→
    55→def _is_triage_stage(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    56→    """True when item is a triage workflow stage."""
    57→    return (
    58→        item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage"
    59→        and str(item.get("id", "")).startswith("triage::")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→
    63→def _is_force_visible(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    64→    """True when the item is explicitly escalated past objective gating."""
    65→    return bool(item.get("force_visible"))
    66→
    67→
    68→def apply_lifecycle_filter(items: list[WorkQueueItem]) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
    69→    """Enforce lifecycle visibility rules."""
    70→    if _has_initial_reviews(items):
    71→        return [
    72→            item for item in items
    73→            if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review")
    74→        ]
    75→    # Enforce fixed endgame phase order:
    76→    #   subjective review -> score workflow -> triage
    77→    # When non-initial subjective reruns are pending and objective work is
    78→    # already drained, keep focus on subjective reruns and defer workflow/triage.
    79→    if _has_endgame_subjective(items) and not _has_objective_items(items):
    80→        return [
    81→            item for item in items
    82→            if _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item)
    83→        ]
    84→    if _has_triage_stages(items):
    85→        # Triage should not block while objective queue work still exists.
    86→        if _has_objective_items(items):
    87→            return [
    88→                item for item in items
    89→                if (
    90→                    (not _is_triage_stage(item) or _is_force_visible(item))
    91→                    and (not _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item))
    92→                )
    93→            ]
    94→        return [
    95→            item for item in items
    96→            if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    97→        ]
    98→    if not _has_objective_items(items):
    99→        return items
   100→    return [item for item in items if not _is_endgame_only(item) or _is_force_visible(item)]
   101→
   102→
   103→__all__ = ["apply_lifecycle_filter"]
   104→
```

```
    46→class _ScanPathFromState:
    47→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    48→
    49→
    50→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    51→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    52→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = _ScanPathFromState()
    53→ScanPathOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromState
    54→
    55→
    56→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    57→class QueueBuildOptions:
    58→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    59→
    60→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    61→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    62→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    63→    """
    64→
    65→    # Output control
    66→    count: int | None = 1
    67→    explain: bool = False
    68→
    69→    # Scope filtering
    70→    scan_path: ScanPathOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    71→    scope: str | None = None
    72→    status: str = "open"
    73→    chronic: bool = False
    74→
    75→    # Subjective gating
    76→    include_subjective: bool = True
    77→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    78→
    79→    # Plan integration
    80→    plan: dict | None = None
    81→    include_skipped: bool = False
    82→
    83→    # Pre-computed context (overrides plan)
    84→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    85→
    86→
    87→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    88→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    89→
    90→    items: list[WorkQueueItem]
    91→    total: int
    92→    grouped: dict[str, list[WorkQueueItem]]
    93→    new_ids: set[str]
    94→
    95→
    96→def build_work_queue(
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    *,
    99→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   100→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   101→    """Build a ranked work queue from state issues.
   102→
   103→    Pipeline:
   104→    1. Gather    — issue items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
   105→    2. Score     — estimate impact from dimension headroom
   106→    3. Presort   — stamp plan positions, separate skipped items
   107→    4. Lifecycle — filter endgame-only items when objective work remains
   108→    5. Sort      — rank by impact/confidence, apply plan order
   109→    6. Limit     — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
   110→    """
   111→    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   112→    plan, scan_path, status, threshold = _resolve_inputs(opts, state)
   113→
   114→    # 1. Gather
   115→    items = build_issue_items(
   116→        state, scan_path=scan_path, status_filter=status,
   117→        scope=opts.scope, chronic=opts.chronic,
   118→    )
   119→    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, threshold)
   120→    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, status)
   121→
   122→    # 2. Score
   123→    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   124→
   125→    # 3. Plan-aware ordering (part 1: separate skipped items)
   126→    new_ids, skipped = _plan_presort(items, state, plan)
   127→
   128→    # 4. Lifecycle filter — endgame-only items filtered when objective work remains
   129→    items = apply_lifecycle_filter(items)
   130→
   131→    # 5. Sort & plan post-processing
   132→    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   133→    _plan_postsort(items, skipped, plan, opts)
   134→
   135→    # 6. Finalize
   136→    if not items:
   137→        items += _empty_queue_fallback(plan)
   138→    total = len(items)
   139→    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
   140→        items = items[:opts.count]
   141→    if opts.explain:
   142→        for item in items:
   143→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   144→
   145→    return {
   146→        "items": items,
   147→        "total": total,
   148→        "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"),
   149→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   150→    }
   151→
   152→
   153→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   154→# Pipeline helpers (private to this module)
   155→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   156→
   157→
   158→def _resolve_inputs(
   159→    opts: QueueBuildOptions, state: StateModel,
   160→) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None, str, float]:
   161→    """Resolve plan, scan_path, status, and subjective threshold from options."""
   162→    ctx = opts.context
   163→    plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else opts.plan
   164→
   165→    scan_path: str | None = (
   166→        state.get("scan_path")
   167→        if isinstance(opts.scan_path, _ScanPathFromState)
   168→        else opts.scan_path
   169→    )
   170→
   171→    status = opts.status
   172→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   173→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   174→
   175→    try:
   176→        threshold = float(opts.subjective_threshold)
   177→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   178→        threshold = 100.0
   179→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, threshold))
   180→
   181→    return plan, scan_path, status, threshold
   182→
   183→
   184→def _gather_subjective_items(
   185→    state: StateModel,
   186→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   187→    threshold: float,
   188→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   189→    """Build subjective dimension candidates.
   190→
   191→    Lifecycle filtering (endgame gating) happens in ``apply_lifecycle_filter``,
   192→    not here. This function only handles configuration and scope.
   193→    """
   194→    if not opts.include_subjective:
   195→        return []
   196→    if opts.status not in {"open", "all"}:
   197→        return []
   198→    if opts.chronic:
   199→        return []
   200→
   201→    candidates = build_subjective_items(
   202→        state, state.get("issues", {}), threshold=threshold,
   203→    )
   204→    return [item for item in candidates if scope_matches(item, opts.scope)]
   205→
   206→
   207→def _gather_workflow_items(
   208→    state: StateModel, plan: dict | None, status: str,
   209→) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   210→    """Inject triage stages, checkpoints, and create-plan when plan is active."""
   211→    if not plan or status not in {"open", "all"}:
   212→        return []
   213→
   214→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = list(build_triage_stage_items(plan, state))
   215→    for builder in (
   216→        build_score_checkpoint_item,
   217→        build_import_scores_item,
   218→        build_communicate_score_item,
   219→    ):
   220→        item = builder(plan, state)
   221→        if item is not None:
   222→            items.append(item)
   223→    plan_item = build_create_plan_item(plan)
   224→    if plan_item is not None:
   225→        items.append(plan_item)
   226→    return items
   227→
   228→
   229→
   230→def _plan_presort(
   231→    items: list[WorkQueueItem], state: StateModel, plan: dict | None,
   232→) -> tuple[set[str], list[WorkQueueItem]]:
   233→    """Enrich plan metadata and stamp sort keys before sorting.
   234→
   235→    Returns ``(new_ids, skipped)`` — skipped items are removed from
   236→    ``items`` in place and returned separately for post-sort re-append.
   237→    """
   238→    if not plan:
   239→        return set(), []
   240→
   241→    new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   242→    enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   243→    stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)
   244→    remaining, skipped = separate_skipped(items, plan)
   245→    items[:] = remaining
   246→    return new_ids, skipped
   247→
   248→
   249→def _plan_postsort(
   250→    items: list[WorkQueueItem],
   251→    skipped: list[WorkQueueItem],
   252→    plan: dict | None,
   253→    opts: QueueBuildOptions,
   254→) -> None:
   255→    """Re-append skipped items and stamp positions.
   256→
   257→    Cluster focus filtering is intentionally NOT applied here — it is a
   258→    view-layer concern that callers apply after building the canonical queue.
   259→    This prevents UI focus state from affecting lifecycle decisions (scan
   260→    gating, score display mode, empty-queue fallback).
   261→    """
   262→    if not plan:
   263→        return
   264→
   265→    if opts.include_skipped:
   266→        items.extend(skipped)
   267→    stamp_positions(items, plan)
   268→
   269→
   270→def _empty_queue_fallback(plan: dict | None) -> list[WorkQueueItem]:
   271→    """Return end-of-queue workflow actions when no active queue items remain."""
   272→    if not plan:
   273→        return []
   274→
   275→    items: list[WorkQueueItem] = []
   276→    deferred_item = build_deferred_disposition_item(plan)
   277→    if deferred_item is not None:
   278→        items.append(deferred_item)
   279→        return items
   280→
   281→    if not postflight_scan_pending(plan):
   282→        return items
   283→
   284→    items.append({
   285→        "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   286→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   287→        "summary": "Queue cleared - run scan to refresh and surface follow-up review work.",
   288→        "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   289→        "file": "",
   290→        "detector": "workflow",
   291→        "confidence": "high",
   292→    })
   293→    return items
   294→
```

```
1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
    14→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→
    19→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    20→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    21→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    22→})
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    27→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    28→
    29→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    30→    objective_count: int  # how many
    31→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    32→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    33→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    34→
    35→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    36→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    37→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    38→            return True  # unconditional
    39→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    40→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    41→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    42→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    43→        return False
    44→
    45→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    46→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    47→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    48→            return False  # never evict unscored
    49→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    50→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    51→        return False
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    55→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    56→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    57→
    58→
    59→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    61→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    62→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    63→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    64→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    65→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    66→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    67→            return True
    68→    return False
    69→
    70→
    71→class _ScanPathFromStatePolicy:
    72→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    73→
    74→
    75→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = _ScanPathFromStatePolicy()
    76→ScanPathPolicyOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromStatePolicy
    77→
    78→
    79→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    83→    scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    84→    plan: dict | None = None,
    85→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    86→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    87→
    88→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    89→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    90→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    91→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    92→
    93→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    94→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import (
    97→        current_stale_ids,
    98→        current_under_target_ids,
    99→        current_unscored_ids,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   103→        state.get("scan_path")
   104→        if isinstance(scan_path, _ScanPathFromStatePolicy)
   105→        else scan_path
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   109→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   110→
   111→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   112→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   113→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   114→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   115→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   116→    objective_count = sum(
   117→        1
   118→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   119→        if issue.get("status") == Status.OPEN
   120→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   121→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   122→        and not _is_evidence_only(issue)
   123→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(issue.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   124→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   128→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   129→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   132→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   133→        objective_count=objective_count,
   134→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   135→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   136→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   142→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   143→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   144→]
   145→
```

```
    29→def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:

    33→        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
    34→    return refresh_state

    43→def _latest_assessment_audit(

    49→    if not isinstance(audit, list):
    50→        return None
    51→    for entry in reversed(audit):
    52→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    53→            continue
    54→        mode = entry.get("mode")
    55→        if isinstance(mode, str) and mode in modes:
    56→            return entry
    57→    return None

    71→        if isinstance(raw_provenance, dict):
    72→            provenance = raw_provenance
    73→        raw_assessments = import_payload.get("assessments")
    74→        if isinstance(raw_assessments, dict):

    76→        raw_issues = import_payload.get("issues")

   106→def pending_import_scores_meta(

   113→    meta = refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   114→    if isinstance(meta, dict) and meta:
   115→        return meta
   116→    issues_only_audit = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   117→    if issues_only_audit is None:

   119→    return _build_pending_import_scores_meta(

   126→def import_scores_meta_matches(
   127→    meta: dict[str, Any] | None,
   128→    *,

   130→    import_payload: dict[str, Any],
   131→) -> tuple[bool, list[str]]:
   132→    """Return whether the current import matches the pending score-import batch."""
   133→    if not isinstance(meta, dict) or not meta:
   134→        return True, []
   135→
   136→    mismatches: list[str] = []
   137→    expected_file = str(meta.get("normalized_import_file", "")).strip()
   138→    current_file = _normalize_match_path(import_file) or ""
   139→    if expected_file and current_file != expected_file:
   140→        mismatches.append(
   141→            f"expected import file {meta.get('import_file')}, got {import_file}"
   142→        )
   143→
   144→    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
   145→    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
   146→    expected_hash = str(meta.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   147→    current_hash = str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   148→    if expected_hash and current_hash != expected_hash:
   149→        mismatches.append(
   150→            f"expected packet_sha256 {expected_hash}, got {current_hash or '<missing>'}"
   151→        )
   152→
   153→    expected_packet_path = str(meta.get("normalized_packet_path", "")).strip()
   154→    current_packet_path = _normalize_match_path(provenance_dict.get("packet_path")) or ""
   155→    if expected_packet_path and current_packet_path != expected_packet_path:
   156→        mismatches.append(
   157→            "expected packet_path "
   158→            f"{meta.get('packet_path')}, got {provenance_dict.get('packet_path') or '<missing>'}"
   159→        )
   160→
   161→    expected_dims = meta.get("assessment_dimensions") or []
   162→    if expected_dims:
   163→        assessments = import_payload.get("assessments")
   164→        assessment_keys = sorted(
   165→            key.strip()
   166→            for key in (assessments if isinstance(assessments, dict) else {}).keys()
   167→            if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   168→        )
   169→        if assessment_keys != expected_dims:
   170→            mismatches.append(
   171→                f"expected assessment dimensions {expected_dims}, got {assessment_keys}"
   172→            )
   173→    return not mismatches, mismatches

   180→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)

   196→def _inject(plan: PlanModel, item_id: str) -> QueueSyncResult:
   197→    """Inject *item_id* into the workflow prefix and clear stale skip entries."""
   198→    order = plan["queue_order"]
   199→    if item_id not in order:
   200→        order.append(item_id)
   201→    normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(order)
   202→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   203→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
   204→        skipped.pop(item_id, None)
   205→    return QueueSyncResult(injected=[item_id])

   212→    plan: PlanModel,

   216→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   217→    """Inject ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` when all initial reviews complete.
   218→
   219→    Injects when:
   220→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   221→    - ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` is not already in the queue
   222→
   223→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   224→    """
   225→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   226→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   227→
   228→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID in order:
   229→        return _EMPTY()
   230→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   231→        return _EMPTY()
   232→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID)
```
