   235→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   236→    plan: PlanModel,
   237→    state: StateModel,
   238→    *,
   239→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   240→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   241→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   242→
   243→    Only injects when:
   244→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   245→    - At least one objective issue exists
   246→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   247→    - No triage stages are pending
   248→
   249→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   250→    """
   251→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   252→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   253→
   254→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   255→        return _EMPTY()
   256→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   257→        return _EMPTY()
   258→    if not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   259→        return _EMPTY()
   260→
   261→    if not has_objective_backlog(state, policy):
   262→        return _EMPTY()
   263→
   264→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   265→
   266→
   267→def sync_import_scores_needed(
   268→    plan: PlanModel,
   269→    state: StateModel,
   270→    *,
   271→    assessment_mode: str | None = None,
   272→    import_file: str | None = None,
   273→    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   274→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   275→    """Inject ``workflow::import-scores`` after issues-only import.
   276→
   277→    Only injects when:
   278→    - Assessment mode was ``issues_only`` (scores were skipped)
   279→    - ``workflow::import-scores`` is not already in the queue
   280→
   281→    Front-loads it into the workflow prefix so it stays ahead of triage.
   282→    """
   283→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   284→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   285→    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
   286→    pending_meta = refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
   287→    latest_issues_only = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
   288→    latest_trusted = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes=_TRUSTED_ASSESSMENT_MODES)
   289→
   290→    stale_pending = False
   291→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   292→        if latest_issues_only is None:
   293→            stale_pending = True
   294→        elif latest_trusted is not None:
   295→            latest_issues_ts = str(latest_issues_only.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   296→            latest_trusted_ts = str(latest_trusted.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   297→            pending_ts = ""
   298→            if isinstance(pending_meta, dict):
   299→                pending_ts = str(pending_meta.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
   300→            compare_ts = pending_ts or latest_issues_ts
   301→            if compare_ts and latest_trusted_ts and latest_trusted_ts >= compare_ts:
   302→                stale_pending = True
   303→    if stale_pending:
   304→        _clear_pending_import_scores(plan)
   305→        return QueueSyncResult(pruned=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   306→
   307→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
   308→        if assessment_mode == "issues_only":
   309→            # Update metadata to track the latest issues-only batch
   310→            refresh_state[_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY] = _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   311→                import_file=import_file,
   312→                import_payload=import_payload,
   313→                issues_only_audit=latest_issues_only,
   314→            )
   315→            return QueueSyncResult(resurfaced=[WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID])
   316→        return _EMPTY()
   317→    if assessment_mode != "issues_only":
   318→        return _EMPTY()
   319→    result = _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID)
   320→    refresh_state[_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY] = _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
   321→        import_file=import_file,
   322→        import_payload=import_payload,
   323→        issues_only_audit=latest_issues_only,
   324→    )
   325→    return result
   326→
   327→
   328→class ScoreSnapshot:
   329→    """Minimal score snapshot for rebaseline — avoids importing state.py."""
   330→
   331→    __slots__ = ("strict", "overall", "objective", "verified")
   332→
   333→    def __init__(
   334→        self,
   335→        *,
   336→        strict: float | None,
   337→        overall: float | None,
   338→        objective: float | None,
   339→        verified: float | None,
   340→    ) -> None:
   341→        self.strict = strict
   342→        self.overall = overall
   343→        self.objective = objective
   344→        self.verified = verified
   345→
   346→
   347→def sync_communicate_score_needed(
   348→    plan: PlanModel,
   349→    state: StateModel,
   350→    *,
   351→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   352→    scores_just_imported: bool = False,
   353→    current_scores: ScoreSnapshot | None = None,
   354→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   355→    """Inject ``workflow::communicate-score`` and rebaseline scores.
   356→
   357→    Injects when:
   358→    - All initial subjective reviews are complete (no unscored dims), OR
   359→      scores were just imported (trusted/attested/override)
   360→    - ``workflow::communicate-score`` is not already in the queue
   361→    - Score has not already been communicated this cycle
   362→      (``previous_plan_start_scores`` absent), unless a trusted score import
   363→      explicitly refreshed the live score mid-cycle
   364→
   365→    When injected and *current_scores* is provided, ``plan_start_scores``
   366→    is rebaselined to the current score so the score display unfreezes at
   367→    the new value.  The previous baseline is preserved in
   368→    ``previous_plan_start_scores`` so the communicate-score queue item can
   369→    show the old → new delta — and so mid-cycle scans know not to
   370→    re-inject.
   371→    """
   372→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   373→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   374→
   375→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in order:
   376→        return _EMPTY()
   377→    # Already communicated this cycle — previous_plan_start_scores is set
   378→    # at injection time and cleared at cycle boundaries.
   379→    if "previous_plan_start_scores" in plan and not scores_just_imported:
   380→        return _EMPTY()
   381→    if not scores_just_imported and not _no_unscored(state, policy):
   382→        return _EMPTY()
   383→
   384→    if current_scores is not None:
   385→        _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(plan, current_scores)
   386→    # Set sentinel even when rebaseline was a no-op (no plan_start_scores
   387→    # to rebaseline) so mid-cycle scans don't re-inject.
   388→    if not plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores"):
   389→        plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = {}
   390→    return _inject(plan, WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID)
   391→
   392→
   393→def _rebaseline_plan_start_scores(
   394→    plan: PlanModel,
   395→    scores: ScoreSnapshot,
   396→) -> None:
   397→    """Snapshot the current score as the new baseline, preserving the old one."""
   398→    old_start = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   399→    if not isinstance(old_start, dict) or not old_start:
   400→        return
   401→    if scores.strict is None:
   402→        return
   403→
   404→    plan["previous_plan_start_scores"] = dict(old_start)
   405→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   406→        "strict": scores.strict,
   407→        "overall": scores.overall,
   408→        "objective": scores.objective,
   409→        "verified": scores.verified,
   410→    }
   411→
   412→
   413→__all__ = [
   414→    "ScoreSnapshot",
   415→    "import_scores_meta_matches",
   416→    "pending_import_scores_meta",
   417→    "sync_communicate_score_needed",
   418→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   419→    "sync_import_scores_needed",
   420→    "sync_score_checkpoint_needed",
   421→]
   422→

1→"""Shared constants and helpers for plan internals."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
     9→
    10→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    11→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    12→
    13→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    14→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    15→    "triage::observe",
    16→    "triage::reflect",
    17→    "triage::organize",
    18→    "triage::enrich",
    19→    "triage::sense-check",
    20→    "triage::commit",
    21→)
    22→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    23→_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES = {
    24→    stage_id.removeprefix("triage::") for stage_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
    25→}
    26→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    27→WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID = "workflow::score-checkpoint"
    28→WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID = "workflow::import-scores"
    29→WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID = "workflow::communicate-score"
    30→WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID = "workflow::deferred-disposition"
    31→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    32→WORKFLOW_IDS = {
    33→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    34→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    35→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    36→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    37→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    38→}
    39→WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER = (
    40→    WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
    41→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    42→    WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
    43→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
    44→    WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
    45→)
    46→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    47→
    48→
    49→@dataclass
    50→class QueueSyncResult:
    51→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    52→
    53→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    56→    deferred: bool = False
    57→
    58→    @property
    59→    def changes(self) -> int:
    60→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)
    61→
    62→
    63→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    64→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    65→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    66→        return {}
    67→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    68→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    69→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    70→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    71→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    72→        return meta_or_stages
    73→    return {}
    74→
    75→
    76→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
    78→    return {
    79→        str(name)
    80→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    81→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
    82→    }
    83→
    84→
    85→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
    87→    return {
    88→        str(name)
    89→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    90→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
    91→    }
    92→
    93→
    94→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
    95→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
    96→    seen: set[str] = set()
    97→    normalized: list[str] = []
    98→
    99→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   100→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   101→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   102→            seen.add(issue_id)
   103→
   104→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   105→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   106→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   107→            seen.add(issue_id)
   108→
   109→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   110→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   111→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   112→            seen.add(issue_id)
   113→
   114→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   115→        if issue_id in seen:
   116→            continue
   117→        normalized.append(issue_id)
   118→        seen.add(issue_id)
   119→
   120→    queue_order[:] = normalized
   121→
   122→
   123→__all__ = [
   124→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   125→    "QueueSyncResult",
   126→    "normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix",
   127→    "confirmed_triage_stage_names",

1→"""Workflow-specific synthetic work queue item builders."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import shlex
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.plan_queue import (
     8→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,

    16→    triage_manual_stage_command,
    17→    triage_run_stages_command,
    18→    triage_runner_commands,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.types import WorkQueueItem
    21→
    22→_TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = (
    23→    ("observe", "triage::observe"),
    24→    ("reflect", "triage::reflect"),
    25→    ("organize", "triage::organize"),
    26→    ("enrich", "triage::enrich"),
    27→    ("sense-check", "triage::sense-check"),
    28→)
    29→
    30→
    31→def _confirm_attestation_hint(stage: str) -> str:
    32→    if stage == "observe":
    33→        return "I have thoroughly reviewed..."
    34→    if stage == "reflect":
    35→        return "My strategy accounts for..."
    36→    if stage == "organize":
    37→        return "This plan is correct..."
    38→    if stage == "enrich":
    39→        return "Steps are executor-ready..."
    40→    if stage == "sense-check":
    41→        return "Content and structure verified..."
    42→    return "..."
    43→
    44→
    45→def _runner_planning_tools(
    46→    *,
    47→    only_stages: str | None = None,
    48→    manual_fallback: str | None = None,
    49→) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    50→    tools = [
    51→        {"label": label, "command": command}
    52→        for label, command in triage_runner_commands(only_stages=only_stages)
    53→    ]
    54→    if manual_fallback:
    55→        tools.append({"label": "Manual fallback", "command": manual_fallback})
    56→    tools.append({"label": "Dashboard", "command": "desloppify plan triage"})
    57→    return tools
    58→
    59→
    60→def _triage_progression_target(plan: dict) -> tuple[str | None, bool]:
    61→    """Return current triage stage target and whether it requires confirmation."""
    62→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    63→    triage_stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {}) or {}
    64→    for stage, _sid in _TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    65→        stage_payload = triage_stages.get(stage)
    66→        if isinstance(stage_payload, dict) and stage_payload and not stage_payload.get("confirmed_at"):
    67→            return stage, True
    68→
    69→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    70→    for stage, sid in _TRIAGE_STAGE_SPECS:
    71→        if sid not in order:
    72→            continue
    73→        return stage, False
    74→
    75→    if "triage::commit" in order:
    76→        return "commit", False
    77→
    78→    return None, False
    79→
    80→
    81→def _create_plan_primary_command(plan: dict) -> str:
    82→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
    83→    if stage is None:
    84→        return triage_run_stages_command()
    85→    if needs_confirm:
    86→        attestation = _confirm_attestation_hint(stage)
    87→        return (
    88→            f'desloppify plan triage --confirm {stage} '
    89→            f'--attestation "{attestation}"'
    90→        )
    91→    if stage == "commit":
    92→        return triage_run_stages_command()
    93→    return triage_run_stages_command(only_stages=stage)
    94→
    95→
    96→def _create_plan_planning_tools(plan: dict) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    97→    stage, needs_confirm = _triage_progression_target(plan)
    98→    if needs_confirm:
    99→        return []
   100→    if stage == "commit":
   101→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   102→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("commit"),
   103→        )
   104→    if stage:
   105→        return _runner_planning_tools(
   106→            only_stages=stage,
   107→            manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command(stage),
   108→        )
   109→    return _runner_planning_tools(
   110→        only_stages="observe",
   111→        manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   112→    )
   113→
   114→
   115→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   116→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::score-checkpoint`` if queued."""
   117→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   118→        return None
   119→
   120→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   121→
   122→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   123→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   124→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   125→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   126→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   127→
   128→    return {
   129→        "id": WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID,
   130→        "tier": 1,
   131→        "confidence": "high",
   132→        "detector": "workflow",
   133→        "file": ".",
   134→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   135→        "summary": f"Score checkpoint: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   136→        "detail": {
   137→            "strict": strict,
   138→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   139→            "delta": delta,
   140→            "planning_tools": _runner_planning_tools(
   141→                only_stages="observe",
   142→                manual_fallback=triage_manual_stage_command("observe"),
   143→            ),
   144→        },
   145→        "primary_command": triage_run_stages_command(only_stages="observe"),
   146→        "blocked_by": [],
   147→        "is_blocked": False,
   148→    }
   149→
   150→
   151→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   152→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if queued."""
   153→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   154→        return None
   155→
   156→    return {
   157→        "id": WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   158→        "tier": 1,
   159→        "confidence": "high",
   160→        "detector": "workflow",
   161→        "file": ".",
   162→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   163→        "summary": "Create prioritized plan from review results",
   164→        "detail": {
   165→            "planning_tools": _create_plan_planning_tools(plan),
   166→        },
   167→        "primary_command": _create_plan_primary_command(plan),
   168→        "blocked_by": [],
   169→        "is_blocked": False,
   170→    }
   171→
   172→
   173→def build_import_scores_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   174→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::import-scores`` if queued."""
   175→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   176→        return None
   177→    meta = pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state) or {}
   178→    import_file = str(meta.get("import_file", "")).strip() or "issues.json"
   179→    quoted_import_file = shlex.quote(import_file)
   180→    packet_sha = str(meta.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
   181→    packet_path = str(meta.get("packet_path", "")).strip()
   182→    dims = meta.get("assessment_dimensions") or []
   183→    explanation = (
   184→        "Review issues were imported but assessment scores were skipped "
   185→        "(untrusted source). Re-import the same batch with attestation to update dimension scores."
   186→    )
   187→    if import_file != "issues.json":
   188→        explanation += f" Expected import file: `{import_file}`."
   189→    if packet_sha:
   190→        explanation += f" Expected packet sha256: `{packet_sha}`."
   191→    if packet_path:
   192→        explanation += f" Blind packet: `{packet_path}`."
   193→    if dims:
   194→        explanation += f" Assessment dimensions: {', '.join(dims)}."
   195→
   196→    return {
   197→        "id": WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
   198→        "tier": 1,
   199→        "confidence": "high",
   200→        "detector": "workflow",
   201→        "file": ".",
   202→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   203→        "summary": "Import assessment scores with attestation",
   204→        "detail": {
   205→            "explanation": explanation,
   206→            "expected_import_file": import_file,
   207→            "packet_sha256": packet_sha,
   208→            "packet_path": packet_path,
   209→            "assessment_dimensions": dims,
   210→        },
   211→        "primary_command": (
   212→            f"desloppify review --import {quoted_import_file} --attested-external "
   213→            '--attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness '
   214→            'of overall score and is unbiased."'
   215→        ),
   216→        "blocked_by": [],
   217→        "is_blocked": False,
   218→    }
   219→
   220→
   221→def build_communicate_score_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   222→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::communicate-score`` if queued."""
   223→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   224→        return None
   225→
   226→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   227→
   228→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   229→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   230→
   231→    # Use previous_plan_start_scores (stashed at rebaseline) for the delta
   232→    # so the user sees old-baseline → current, not current → current.
   233→    prev_start = (plan.get("previous_plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   234→    if prev_start is None:
   235→        prev_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   236→    delta = round(strict - prev_start, 1) if prev_start is not None else None
   237→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   238→
   239→    return {
   240→        "id": WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   241→        "tier": 1,
   242→        "confidence": "high",
   243→        "detector": "workflow",
   244→        "file": ".",
   245→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   246→        "summary": f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   247→        "detail": {
   248→            "strict": strict,
   249→            "previous_plan_start_strict": prev_start,
   250→            "delta": delta,
   251→        },
   252→        "primary_command": (
   253→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   254→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   255→        ),
   256→        "blocked_by": [],
   257→        "is_blocked": False,
   258→    }
   259→
   260→
   261→def _temporary_skipped_ids(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   262→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   263→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
   264→        return []
   265→    deferred: list[str] = []
   266→    for issue_id, entry in skipped.items():
   267→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   268→            continue
   269→        if str(entry.get("kind", "temporary")) == "temporary":
   270→            deferred.append(str(issue_id))
   271→    deferred.sort()
   272→    return deferred
   273→
   274→
   275→def _deferred_cluster_breakdown(
   276→    plan: dict,
   277→    deferred_ids: list[str],
   278→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   279→    deferred_set = set(deferred_ids)
   280→    if not deferred_set:
   281→        return 0, 0
   282→
   283→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   284→    if not isinstance(clusters, dict):
   285→        return 0, len(deferred_ids)
   286→
   287→    covered_by_cluster: set[str] = set()
   288→    cluster_count = 0
   289→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   290→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   291→            continue
   292→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
   293→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
   294→            continue
   295→        matched = {str(issue_id) for issue_id in issue_ids if str(issue_id) in deferred_set}
   296→        if not matched:
   297→            continue
   298→        cluster_count += 1
   299→        covered_by_cluster.update(matched)
   300→
   301→    individual_count = len(deferred_set - covered_by_cluster)
   302→    return cluster_count, individual_count
   303→
   304→
   305→def build_deferred_disposition_item(plan: dict) -> WorkQueueItem | None:
   306→    """Build a synthetic item prompting deferred backlog disposition."""
   307→    deferred_ids = _temporary_skipped_ids(plan)
   308→    if not deferred_ids:
   309→        return None
   310→
   311→    count = len(deferred_ids)
   312→    cluster_count, individual_count = _deferred_cluster_breakdown(plan, deferred_ids)
   313→    cluster_label = "cluster" if cluster_count == 1 else "clusters"
   314→    individual_label = "item" if individual_count == 1 else "items"
   315→    reactivate_cmd = 'desloppify plan unskip "*"'
   316→    subset_reactivate_cmd = "desloppify plan unskip <cluster-or-id>"
   317→    inspect_cmd = "desloppify plan queue --include-skipped"
   318→    inspect_item_cmd = "desloppify show <cluster-or-id>"
   319→    wontfix_cmd = (
   320→        'desloppify plan skip --permanent "*" '
   321→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   322→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently." '
   323→        "--confirm"
   324→    )
   325→    subset_wontfix_cmd = (
   326→        "desloppify plan skip --permanent <cluster-or-id> "
   327→        '--note "<why this deferred work should stay wontfix>" '
   328→        '--attest "I have actually reviewed these deferred items and I am not gaming the score by skipping them permanently."'
   329→    )
   330→
   331→    return {
   332→        "id": WORKFLOW_DEFERRED_DISPOSITION_ID,
   333→        "tier": 1,
   334→        "confidence": "high",
   335→        "detector": "workflow",
   336→        "file": ".",
   337→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   338→        "summary": (
   339→            "Deferred backlog decision required: "
   340→            f"{cluster_count} {cluster_label} + {individual_count} individual {individual_label} "
   341→            "must be reactivated or marked wontfix."
   342→        ),
   343→        "detail": {
   344→            "temporary_skipped_count": count,
   345→            "deferred_cluster_count": cluster_count,
   346→            "deferred_individual_count": individual_count,
   347→            "reactivate_command": reactivate_cmd,
   348→            "wontfix_command": wontfix_cmd,
   349→            "planning_tools": [
   350→                {
   351→                    "label": "Review deferred backlog",
   352→                    "command": inspect_cmd,
   353→                },
   354→                {
   355→                    "label": "Inspect a specific cluster or item",
   356→                    "command": inspect_item_cmd,
   357→                },
   358→                {
   359→                    "label": "Reactivate a subset",
   360→                    "command": subset_reactivate_cmd,
   361→                },
   362→                {
   363→                    "label": "Mark a subset as permanent wontfix",
   364→                    "command": subset_wontfix_cmd,
   365→                },
   366→            ],
   367→            "decision_options": [
   368→                {
   369→                    "label": "Reactivate deferred work",
   370→                    "command": reactivate_cmd,
   371→                },
   372→                {
   373→                    "label": "Convert deferred work to wontfix",
   374→                    "command": wontfix_cmd,
   375→                },
   376→            ],
   377→        },
   378→        "primary_command": reactivate_cmd,
   379→        "blocked_by": [],
   380→        "is_blocked": False,
   381→    }
   382→
   383→
   384→__all__ = [
   385→    "build_communicate_score_item",

   387→    "build_deferred_disposition_item",
   388→    "build_import_scores_item",

    19→def _print_review_import_sync(
    20→    state: dict,
    21→    result: plan_queue_mod.ReviewImportSyncResult,
    22→    *,
    23→    workflow_injected: bool,
    24→) -> None:
    25→    """Print summary of plan changes after review import sync."""
    26→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    27→    stale_pruned = result.stale_pruned_from_queue
    28→    print()
    29→    if new_ids:
    30→        print(colorize(
    31→            f"  Plan updated: {len(new_ids)} new review issue(s) added to queue.",
    32→            "bold",
    33→        ))
    34→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
    35→        for finding_id in sorted(new_ids)[:10]:
    36→            finding = issues.get(finding_id, {})
    37→            print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(finding_id)}] {finding.get('summary', '')}")
    38→        if len(new_ids) > 10:
    39→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 10} more", "dim"))
    40→    if stale_pruned:
    41→        print(colorize(
    42→            f"  Plan updated: {len(stale_pruned)} stale review issue(s) removed from queue.",
    43→            "bold",
    44→        ))
    45→    print()
    46→    print(colorize(
    47→        "  Review queue sync completed. Workflow follow-up may be front-loaded.",
    48→        "dim",
    49→    ))
    50→    print()
    51→    print(colorize("  View queue:            desloppify plan queue", "dim"))
    52→    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
    53→    print()
    54→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP:", "yellow"))
    55→    print(colorize("    Run:    desloppify next", "yellow"))
    56→    if result.triage_injected and not workflow_injected:
    57→        print(colorize(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}", "dim"))
    58→        print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
    59→        print(colorize("    Manual dashboard: desloppify plan triage", "dim"))
    60→    print(colorize(
    61→        "  (Follow the queue in order; score communication and planning come before triage.)",

    63→    ))

    66→def sync_plan_after_import(
    67→    state: dict,
    68→    diff: dict,
    69→    assessment_mode: str,
    70→    *,
    71→    state_file: str | Path | None = None,
    72→    config: dict | None = None,
    73→    import_file: str | None = None,
    74→    import_payload: dict | None = None,
    75→) -> None:
    76→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
    77→    try:
    78→        plan_path = None
    79→        if state_file is not None:
    80→            plan_path = plan_queue_mod.plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
    81→        if not plan_queue_mod.has_living_plan(plan_path):
    82→            return
    83→
    84→        plan = plan_queue_mod.load_plan(plan_path)
    85→        dirty = False
    86→        workflow_injected_ids: list[str] = []
    87→        policy = plan_queue_mod.compute_subjective_visibility(

    89→            target_strict=target_strict_score_from_config(config),
    90→            plan=plan,
    91→        )
    92→
    93→        snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    94→        current_scores = plan_queue_mod.ScoreSnapshot(
    95→            strict=snapshot.strict,
    96→            overall=snapshot.overall,
    97→            objective=snapshot.objective,
    98→            verified=snapshot.verified,
    99→        )
   100→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   101→
   102→        communicate_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_communicate_score_needed(
   103→            plan,
   104→            state,
   105→            policy=policy,
   106→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
   107→            current_scores=current_scores,
   108→        )
   109→        if communicate_result.changes:
   110→            dirty = True
   111→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::communicate-score")
   112→
   113→        has_review_issue_delta = (
   114→            int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0) > 0
   115→            or int(diff.get("reopened", 0) or 0) > 0
   116→            or int(diff.get("auto_resolved", 0) or 0) > 0
   117→        )
   118→        import_result = None
   119→        covered_ids: list[str] = []
   120→        stale_sync_result = None
   121→
   122→        injected_parts: list[str] = []
   123→        if communicate_result.changes:
   124→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::communicate-score`")
   125→
   126→        import_scores_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_import_scores_needed(

   130→            import_file=import_file,
   131→            import_payload=import_payload,
   132→        )
   133→        if import_scores_result.changes:

   136→                workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::import-scores")
   137→                injected_parts.append("`workflow::import-scores`")
   138→
   139→        create_plan_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_create_plan_needed(

   141→            state,
   142→            policy=policy,
   143→        )
   144→        if create_plan_result.changes:
   145→            dirty = True
   146→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::create-plan")
   147→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::create-plan`")
   148→
   149→        if has_review_issue_delta:
   150→            import_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_plan_after_review_import(
   151→                plan,
   152→                state,
   153→                policy=policy,
   154→            )
   155→            if import_result is not None:
   156→                dirty = True
   157→
   158→            # Keep stale/under-target subjective queue entries aligned with the
   159→            # latest imported assessments/issues. Importing review findings for
   160→            # one dimension must not globally evict other subjective dimensions.
   161→            stale_sync_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_stale_dimensions(
   162→                plan,
   163→                state,
   164→                policy=policy,
   165→            )
   166→            if stale_sync_result.changes:
   167→                dirty = True
   168→
   169→        if dirty:
   170→            if communicate_result.changes:
   171→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(

   176→                )
   177→            if import_scores_result.changes:
   178→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(

   180→                    "sync_import_scores",
   181→                    actor="system",
   182→                    detail={
   183→                        "trigger": "review_import",
   184→                        "injected": bool(import_scores_result.injected),
   185→                        "pruned": list(import_scores_result.pruned),
   186→                    },
   187→                )
   188→            if create_plan_result.changes:
   189→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   190→                    plan,
   191→                    "sync_create_plan",
   192→                    actor="system",
   193→                    detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   194→                )
   195→            if import_result is not None or workflow_injected_ids or covered_ids:
   196→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   197→                    plan,
   198→                    "review_import_sync",
   199→                    actor="system",
   200→                    detail={

   202→                        "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   203→                        "added_to_queue": (
   204→                            import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else []
   205→                        ),
   206→                        "workflow_injected_ids": workflow_injected_ids,
   207→                        "triage_injected": (
   208→                            import_result.triage_injected if import_result is not None else False
   209→                        ),
   210→                        "triage_injected_ids": (
   211→                            import_result.triage_injected_ids if import_result is not None else []
   212→                        ),
   213→                        "triage_deferred": (
   214→                            import_result.triage_deferred if import_result is not None else False
   215→                        ),
   216→                        "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   217→                        "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   218→                        "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   219→                        "stale_pruned_from_queue": (

   222→                        "covered_subjective": covered_ids,
   223→                        "stale_sync_injected": (

   225→                            if stale_sync_result is not None else []

   227→                        "stale_sync_pruned": (

   229→                            if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   230→                        ),
   231→                    },
   232→                )
   233→            plan_queue_mod.save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   234→
   235→        if import_result is not None:
   236→            _print_review_import_sync(

 from __future__ import annotations
 
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
 from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
 from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import (
     SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
@@ -19,6 +22,164 @@ from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
 
 from .context import has_objective_backlog
 
+[REDACTED]
+_TRUSTED_ASSESSMENT_MODES = {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
+
+
+def _refresh_state(plan: PlanModel) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    refresh_state = plan.get("refresh_state")
+    if not isinstance(refresh_state, dict):
+        refresh_state = {}
+        plan["refresh_state"] = refresh_state
+    return refresh_state
+
+
+def _normalize_match_path(raw_path: object) -> str | None:
+    if not isinstance(raw_path, str) or not raw_path.strip():
+        return None
+    return str(Path(raw_path).expanduser().resolve(strict=False))
+
+
+def _latest_assessment_audit(
+    state: StateModel,
+    *,
+    modes: set[str],
+) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
+    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
+    if not isinstance(audit, list):
+        return None
+    for entry in reversed(audit):
+        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
+            continue
+        mode = entry.get("mode")
+        if isinstance(mode, str) and mode in modes:
+            return entry
+    return None
+
+
+def _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
+    *,
+    import_file: str | None,
+    import_payload: dict[str, Any] | None,
+    issues_only_audit: dict[str, Any] | None,
+) -> dict[str, Any]:
+    provenance = {}
+    assessments = {}
+    issues = []
+    if isinstance(import_payload, dict):
+        raw_provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
+        if isinstance(raw_provenance, dict):
+            provenance = raw_provenance
+        raw_assessments = import_payload.get("assessments")
+        if isinstance(raw_assessments, dict):
+            assessments = raw_assessments
+        raw_issues = import_payload.get("issues")
+        if isinstance(raw_issues, list):
+            issues = raw_issues
+    recorded_file = (
+        str(import_file).strip()
+        if isinstance(import_file, str) and import_file.strip()
+        else str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")).strip()
+        if isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict)
+        else ""
+    )
+    timestamp = ""
+    if isinstance(issues_only_audit, dict):
+        timestamp = str(issues_only_audit.get("timestamp", "")).strip()
+    return {
+        "timestamp": timestamp,
+        "import_file": recorded_file,
+        "normalized_import_file": _normalize_match_path(recorded_file),
+        "packet_path": str(provenance.get("packet_path", "")).strip(),
+        "normalized_packet_path": _normalize_match_path(provenance.get("packet_path")),
+        "packet_sha256": str(provenance.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip(),
+        "runner": str(provenance.get("runner", "")).strip(),
+        "assessment_dimensions": sorted(
+            key.strip()
+            for key in assessments.keys()
+            if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
+        ),
+        "issue_count": len(issues),
+    }
+
+
+def pending_import_scores_meta(
+    plan: PlanModel,
+    state: StateModel,
+) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
+    """Return normalized pending score-import metadata, if any."""
+    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
+    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
+    meta = refresh_state.get(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY)
+    if isinstance(meta, dict) and meta:
+        return meta
+    issues_only_audit = _latest_assessment_audit(state, modes={"issues_only"})
+    if issues_only_audit is None:
+        return None
+    return _build_pending_import_scores_meta(
+        import_file=str(issues_only_audit.get("import_file", "")),
+        import_payload=None,
+        issues_only_audit=issues_only_audit,
+    )
+
+
+def import_scores_meta_matches(
+    meta: dict[str, Any] | None,
+    *,
+    import_file: str,
+    import_payload: dict[str, Any],
+) -> tuple[bool, list[str]]:
+    """Return whether the current import matches the pending score-import batch."""
+    if not isinstance(meta, dict) or not meta:
+        return True, []
+
+    mismatches: list[str] = []
+    expected_file = str(meta.get("normalized_import_file", "")).strip()
+    current_file = _normalize_match_path(import_file) or ""
+    if expected_file and current_file != expected_file:
+        mismatches.append(
+            f"expected import file {meta.get('import_file')}, got {import_file}"
+        )
+
+    provenance = import_payload.get("provenance")
+    provenance_dict = provenance if isinstance(provenance, dict) else {}
+    expected_hash = str(meta.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
+    current_hash = str(provenance_dict.get("packet_sha256", "")).strip()
+    if expected_hash and current_hash != expected_hash:
+        mismatches.append(
+            f"expected packet_sha256 {expected_hash}, got {current_hash or '<missing>'}"
+        )
+
+    expected_packet_path = str(meta.get("normalized_packet_path", "")).strip()
+    current_packet_path = _normalize_match_path(provenance_dict.get("packet_path")) or ""
+    if expected_packet_path and current_packet_path != expected_packet_path:
+        mismatches.append(
+            "expected packet_path "
+            f"{meta.get('packet_path')}, got {provenance_dict.get('packet_path') or '<missing>'}"
+        )
+
+    expected_dims = meta.get("assessment_dimensions") or []
+    if expected_dims:
+        assessments = import_payload.get("assessments")
+        assessment_keys = sorted(
+            key.strip()
+            for key in (assessments if isinstance(assessments, dict) else {}).keys()
+            if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
+        )
+        if assessment_keys != expected_dims:
+            mismatches.append(
+                f"expected assessment dimensions {expected_dims}, got {assessment_keys}"
+            )
+    return not mismatches, mismatches
+
+
+def _clear_pending_import_scores(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
+    order = plan["queue_order"]
+    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID in order:
+        order[:] = [item for item in order if item != WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID]
+    refresh_state = _refresh_state(plan)
+    refresh_state.pop(_PENDING_IMPORT_SCORES_KEY, None)
+

1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import os
     8→import shutil
     9→import sys
    10→from collections.abc import Iterator
    11→from contextlib import contextmanager
    12→from pathlib import Path
    13→from typing import cast
    14→
    15→from desloppify.base.discovery.file_paths import safe_write_text
    16→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    18→    PLAN_VERSION,
    19→    PlanModel,
    20→    empty_plan,
    21→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    22→    validate_plan,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    25→    get_state_dir,
    26→    json_default,
    27→    utc_now,
    28→)
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→_PLAN_FILE_SENTINEL = object()
    33→PLAN_FILE = _PLAN_FILE_SENTINEL
    34→_INITIAL_PLAN_FILE = _PLAN_FILE_SENTINEL
    35→
    36→
    37→def get_plan_file() -> Path:
    38→    """Return the default plan file for the current runtime context."""
    39→    return get_state_dir() / "plan.json"
    40→
    41→
    42→def _default_plan_file() -> Path:
    43→    """Resolve the effective default plan path.
    44→
    45→    If tests monkeypatch ``PLAN_FILE`` in this module, use the patched value.
    46→    """
    47→    if PLAN_FILE != _INITIAL_PLAN_FILE:
    48→        return Path(PLAN_FILE)
    49→    return get_plan_file()
    50→
    51→
    52→@contextmanager
    53→def plan_lock(path: Path | None = None) -> Iterator[None]:
    54→    """Acquire exclusive lock on plan file for read-modify-write safety."""
    55→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
    56→    lock_path = plan_path.with_suffix(".lock")
    57→    lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    58→    fd = os.open(str(lock_path), os.O_CREAT | os.O_WRONLY)
    59→    try:
    60→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    61→            import msvcrt
    62→
    63→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_LOCK, 1)
    64→        else:
    65→            import fcntl
    66→
    67→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    68→        yield
    69→    finally:
    70→        if sys.platform == "win32":
    71→            import msvcrt
    72→
    73→            msvcrt.locking(fd, msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
    74→        else:
    75→            import fcntl
    76→
    77→            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    78→        os.close(fd)
    79→
    80→
    81→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    82→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    83→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
    84→    if not plan_path.exists():
    85→        return empty_plan()
    86→
    87→    try:
    88→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    89→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    90→        # Try backup before giving up
    91→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    92→        if backup.exists():
    93→            try:
    94→                data = json.loads(backup.read_text())
    95→                logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s), loaded from backup.", ex)
    96→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.", file=sys.stderr)
    97→                # Fall through to validation below
    98→            except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as backup_ex:
    99→                logger.warning("Plan file and backup both corrupted: %s / %s", ex, backup_ex)
   100→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   101→                return empty_plan()
   102→        else:
   103→            logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
   104→            print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   105→            return empty_plan()
   106→
   107→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
   108→        logger.warning("Plan file root is not a JSON object. Starting fresh.")
   109→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   110→        return empty_plan()
   111→
   112→    version = data.get("version", 1)
   113→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
   114→        logger.warning("Plan file version %d > supported %d.", version, PLAN_VERSION)
   115→        print(
   116→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
   117→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
   118→            file=sys.stderr,
   119→        )
   120→
   121→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
   122→    try:
   123→        validate_plan(data)
   124→    except ValueError as ex:
   125→        logger.warning("Plan invariants invalid (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
   126→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
   127→        return empty_plan()
   128→
   129→    return cast(PlanModel, data)
   130→
   131→
   132→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   133→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
   134→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   135→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
   136→    validate_plan(plan)
   137→
   138→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
   139→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   140→
   141→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
   142→
   143→    if plan_path.exists():
   144→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
   145→        try:
   146→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
   147→        except OSError as backup_ex:
   148→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, "create plan backup", backup_ex)
   149→
   150→    try:
   151→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
   152→    except OSError as ex:
   153→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   154→        raise
   155→
   156→
   157→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
   158→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
   159→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   160→
   161→
   162→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
   163→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
   164→    plan_path = path or _default_plan_file()
   165→    if not plan_path.exists():
   166→        return False
   167→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   168→    return bool(
   169→        plan.get("queue_order")
   170→        or plan.get("overrides")
   171→        or plan.get("clusters")
   172→    )
   173→
   174→
   175→__all__ = [
   176→    "PLAN_FILE",
   177→    "get_plan_file",
   178→    "has_living_plan",
   179→    "load_plan",
   180→    "plan_lock",
   181→    "plan_path_for_state",
   182→    "save_plan",
   183→]
   184→

1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.base.config import (
    14→    DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    15→    target_strict_score_from_config,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    18→from desloppify.engine.plan_queue import (
    19→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    20→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    21→    has_living_plan,
    22→    load_plan,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    25→
    26→
    27→class _PlanAutoLoad:
    28→    """Sentinel type: auto-load plan from disk."""
    29→
    30→
    31→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    32→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = _PlanAutoLoad()
    33→PlanOption = dict | None | _PlanAutoLoad
    34→
    35→
    36→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    37→class PlanLoadStatus:
    38→    """Resolved plan load result with degraded-mode signaling."""
    39→
    40→    plan: dict | None
    41→    degraded: bool
    42→    error_kind: str | None = None
    43→
    44→
    45→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    46→class QueueContext:
    47→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    48→
    49→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through
    50→    ``build_work_queue``, ``plan_aware_queue_breakdown``, and command
    51→    helpers so every call site agrees on plan, target, and policy.
    52→    """
    53→
    54→    plan: dict | None
    55→    target_strict: float
    56→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility
    57→    plan_load_status: PlanLoadStatus
    58→
    59→
    60→def resolve_plan_load_status(
    61→    *,
    62→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    63→) -> PlanLoadStatus:
    64→    """Resolve plan loading and explicitly report degraded mode."""
    65→    if not isinstance(plan, _PlanAutoLoad):
    66→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    67→
    68→    if not has_living_plan():
    69→        return PlanLoadStatus(plan=None, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    70→
    71→    try:
    72→        resolved_plan = load_plan()
    73→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    74→        return PlanLoadStatus(
    75→            plan=None,
    76→            degraded=True,
    77→            error_kind=exc.__class__.__name__,
    78→        )
    79→    return PlanLoadStatus(plan=resolved_plan, degraded=False, error_kind=None)
    80→
    81→
    82→def queue_context(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    config: dict | None = None,
    86→    plan: PlanOption = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    87→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    88→) -> QueueContext:
    89→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    90→
    91→    Resolution order:
    92→
    93→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    94→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    95→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
    96→       via ``target_strict_score_from_config``; final fallback is ``95.0``.
    97→    3. **policy** — ``compute_subjective_visibility(state, ...)`` with the
    98→       resolved plan and target_strict so every downstream consumer sees
    99→       the same objective-vs-subjective balance.
   100→    """
   101→    # --- resolve plan ---
   102→    plan_load_status = resolve_plan_load_status(plan=plan)
   103→    resolved_plan = plan_load_status.plan
   104→
   105→    # --- resolve target_strict ---
   106→    if target_strict is not None:
   107→        resolved_target = target_strict
   108→    elif config is not None:
   109→        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
   110→    else:
   111→        resolved_target = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
   112→
   113→    # --- resolve policy ---
   114→    resolved_policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   115→        state,
   116→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   117→        plan=resolved_plan,
   118→    )
   119→
   120→    return QueueContext(
   121→        plan=resolved_plan,
   122→        target_strict=resolved_target,
   123→        policy=resolved_policy,
   124→        plan_load_status=plan_load_status,
   125→    )
   126→
   127→
   128→__all__ = [
   129→    "PlanLoadStatus",
   130→    "QueueContext",
   131→    "queue_context",
   132→    "resolve_plan_load_status",
   133→]
   134→

Now I have a comprehensive understanding of the system. Let me design the plan.

---

## Implementation Plan: Lifecycle/Visibility Architecture Improvements

### Understanding of the current state

The uncommitted changes on branch `0.9.5` already fix 4 specific bugs:
1. `unclustered_review_issues()` now excludes skipped IDs
2. `sync_plan_after_import` now takes explicit `state_file` and derives plan path from it (no more CWD default)
3. `workflow::import-scores` now stores batch-provenance metadata in `refresh_state["pending_import_scores"]` and validates on re-import
4. `save_plan(plan)` in plan_sync.py now passes explicit `plan_path`

These fixes are tactical. The questions are about the incremental structural improvements that would make future bugs in this class impossible.

### Design Answers and Plan

#### Question 1: Should `_is_endgame_only()` derive from `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS`?

**Answer: Partially, but not as a simple set membership check.**

The current `_is_endgame_only` tests two distinct conditions:
- `detector == "subjective_review"` -- any issue from this detector is endgame-only
- `kind == "subjective_dimension" and not initial_review` -- non-initial subjective dimension reruns

`NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` contains `{"review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment"}`. But `review` and `concerns` items are NOT endgame-only -- they appear alongside objective work. And `subjective_assessment` doesn't produce issues directly (it's the detector name for subjective dimension items, which are handled by the `kind` check).

So mapping `_is_endgame_only` directly to `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` would be wrong. However, the bug that motivated this (subjective_review not being treated as endgame-only) reveals that the relationship between detectors and lifecycle phases is implicit. The fix is not to derive from the set, but to make the classification explicit and testable.

**Proposed approach**: Create a small mapping `ENDGAME_ONLY_DETECTORS` in `lifecycle.py` that is a proper subset of `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS`, and add a module-level assertion that `ENDGAME_ONLY_DETECTORS <= NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS`. This catches the case where someone adds a detector to one set but not the other.

```python
# In lifecycle.py
ENDGAME_ONLY_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"subjective_review"})
assert ENDGAME_ONLY_DETECTORS <= NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS, (
    f"ENDGAME_ONLY_DETECTORS {ENDGAME_ONLY_DETECTORS - NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS} "
    f"not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS"
)

def _is_endgame_only(item: WorkQueueItem) -> bool:
    if item.get("detector", "") in ENDGAME_ONLY_DETECTORS:
        return True
    return (
        item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension"
        and not item.get("initial_review")
    )
```

**Tradeoff**: This is a 5-line change that would have prevented the `subjective_review` bug. It makes the detector-to-phase relationship explicit without over-abstracting. The module-level assertion runs at import time and fails fast.

**Effort**: Minimal. One file change plus test update.

---

#### Question 2: Should workflow items carry `WorkflowItemMeta` instead of storing metadata in `refresh_state`?

**Answer: No, the current approach (storing in `refresh_state`) is better.**

The uncommitted changes already implement the right pattern: batch metadata lives in `plan["refresh_state"]["pending_import_scores"]` as a dict. Reasons the current approach is superior:

1. **Lifecycle alignment**: The metadata must survive across plan load/save cycles. `refresh_state` is part of `PlanModel` and persists. Queue items are ephemeral -- they're rebuilt on every `build_work_queue()` call from state + plan + synthetic builders.

2. **Single source of truth**: If metadata lived on the queue item itself, it would need to be injected in `build_import_scores_item()` and then somehow round-tripped back to the plan. That's the wrong direction of data flow.

3. **The dataclass adds ceremony without safety**: A `WorkflowItemMeta` dataclass would need to be serialized to/from JSON for plan persistence, validated on load, and kept in sync. The current dict-based approach with explicit key names and the `_build_pending_import_scores_meta()` builder function is pragmatically equivalent.

**What IS worth doing**: Formalize the `RefreshState` TypedDict (already started in the schema diff -- `pending_import_scores` is added). The builder function `_build_pending_import_scores_meta` already serves as the factory. Add a test that validates the schema of what gets stored there.

**Effort**: Already done in the uncommitted changes. Just needs test coverage for the metadata round-trip.

---

#### Question 3: Should mutation paths require explicit `PlanContext`?

**Answer: Yes, but scoped to mutation paths only, and implemented incrementally.**

The key insight from the bug: `sync_plan_after_import` called `has_living_plan()` and `load_plan()` with no arguments, falling through to CWD-derived defaults. The fix on the branch passes `state_file` and derives `plan_path` via `plan_path_for_state()`. This is the right pattern.

Looking at the numbers:
- 40 call sites for `load_plan()` (no args)
- 19 call sites for `save_plan(plan)` (no explicit path)
- 4 call sites for `has_living_plan()` (no args)

Splitting these into categories:

**Mutation paths (must fix)** -- places that load, modify, and save the plan:
- `plan_sync.py` (review import) -- FIXED on branch
- `app/commands/plan/triage/workflow.py`
- `app/commands/plan/triage/stage_flow_*.py`
- `app/commands/plan/triage/lifecycle.py`
- `app/commands/plan/triage/confirmations/shared.py`
- `app/commands/plan/reorder_handlers.py`
- `app/commands/plan/override_resolve_*.py`
- `app/commands/plan/cluster_ops_*.py`
- `app/commands/plan/cmd.py`
- `app/commands/plan/commit_log/dispatch.py`
- `app/commands/resolve/living_plan.py`

**Read-only display paths (acceptable with CWD default)** -- places that only load for rendering:
- `engine/planning/render.py`
- `app/commands/show/cmd.py`
- `app/commands/status/flow.py`
- `app/commands/scan/reporting/*.py`
- `engine/_work_queue/context.py` (QueueContext auto-load)

**Proposed approach**: Rather than introducing a full `PlanContext` object, adopt the pattern already established by the fix: every function that does load+mutate+save should accept an explicit `plan_path: Path | None` parameter. The `plan_path_for_state()` derivation should happen at the CLI command entry point (where `state_file` is known) and be threaded down.

The `plan` CLI commands are different -- they receive the plan path from their own argument parsing. Those are already safe because the CWD IS the project root when running `desloppify plan *`.

**Priority**: Focus on the `app/commands/resolve/living_plan.py` path first (it's the one other mutation path that was clearly wrong). The triage stage commands are typically invoked from within the project root, so they're lower risk.

**Effort**: Medium. About 12-15 files need the `plan_path` parameter threaded through. Most are mechanical. Start with `resolve/living_plan.py` and `scan/preflight.py`, then sweep triage.

---

#### Question 4: Is there value in a `QueueItemClassification` enum?

**Answer: Not as a full enum, but a `lifecycle_phase` field on `WorkQueueItem` would be valuable.**

The current lifecycle logic in `apply_lifecycle_filter` works by inference: it examines `kind`, `detector`, and `initial_review` to determine what phase an item belongs to. This works but is fragile -- the `subjective_review` bug was exactly this kind of inference error.

A full enum with every state is over-engineering. But stamping a `lifecycle_phase` field during queue item construction would:
1. Move classification to the point where item provenance is known (the builders)
2. Make `apply_lifecycle_filter` a simple field check instead of heuristic inference
3. Make lifecycle behavior testable in isolation

**Proposed approach**: Add a `lifecycle_phase` field to `WorkQueueItem` with values from a simple string literal type:

```python
LifecyclePhase = Literal["always", "initial_review", "endgame", "triage", "workflow"]
```

Stamp it in the builder functions:
- `build_issue_items` -- `"always"` for objective issues, `"endgame"` for `subjective_review`/`concerns` detectors, `"always"` for `review` detector
- `build_subjective_items` -- `"initial_review"` when `initial_review=True`, else `"endgame"`
- `build_triage_stage_items` -- `"triage"`
- `build_*_item` workflow builders -- `"workflow"`

Then `_is_endgame_only` becomes `item.get("lifecycle_phase") == "endgame"`.

**Tradeoff**: This is a structural improvement but touches more files. It's the right long-term direction but can be deferred until after the immediate bug fixes ship. The assertion-based approach from Question 1 provides the immediate safety net.

**Effort**: Medium-high. Touches all builder functions and lifecycle.py. Should be its own PR.
